羅子評

亞泥(1102) 
區間操作, TP 37 (潛在報酬 10.61%)  
區間操作 
Price(08/17)：33.45 
Target Price：37

亞泥 26Q2 單季營收達 178.31 億元 ，QoQ 20.24%，YoY -4.47%。
雖受中國煤炭成本攀升及報價低迷影響，拖累合併毛利率降至
14.03%；然受惠遠東新與裕民等業外轉投資挹注，單季EPS 仍逆
勢跳增至 1.57 元。展望後市，考量中國水泥市場呈 L 型築底且台
灣靜待房市鬆綁，本業延續承壓基調。研究部預估2026 全年 EPS
為 3.27 元；若以過往 65%配息率推估，2027 年有望配發 2.13 元
現金股利，配息後每股淨值約為 51.02 元。 
評價方面， 考量整體水泥產業仍處於下行階段 ， 且本業營收與獲利
持續承壓，給予過往區間中緣偏下之 0.7 倍 PBR 評價，目標價設
定為 37 元。惟業外獲利強勁有效彌補本業缺口，並提供配息下檔
保護；鑑於公司具備長期殖利率保護優勢，投資建議維持區間操
作，建議可逢低布局。  
⚫ 近期投資評等 ⚫ 公司基本資料 
亞洲水泥隸屬於遠東集團 ， 為台灣與中國具指標性之建材與多
角化控股公司。集團主要營運版圖涵蓋水泥、非水泥及策略轉
投資三大板塊。以近期營收結構分析，核心水泥本業合計貢獻
總營收將近七成，其中台灣市場約占近四成，中國市場約占三
成；非水泥事業則以電力領域為營運第二隻腳，穩定貢獻約兩
成營收，其餘則為不鏽鋼及其他業務。此外，亞泥亦透過權益
法戰略投資遠東新、裕民及山水水泥等關聯企業，建構出具防
禦性且多角化的穩健獲利基礎。

⚫ 中國水泥事業低迷，惟業外貢獻支撐 26Q2 EPS 至 1.57 元 
26Q2 工作天數增加，台灣水泥出貨季增；但中國市場報價偏
低且需求年減，使集團單季營收達 178.31 億元， QoQ 
20.24%，YoY -4.47%。獲利方面，因中國煤炭成本攀升與終
端報價低迷，中國事業噸毛利由 25Q2 的 40 元人民幣降至約
15 元 ， 並轉為虧損 ， 拖累合併毛利率降至14.03%， 年減4.04
個百分點，季增 1.02 個百分點，營業利益 17.11 億元，QoQ 
44.52%，YoY -34.09%。惟業外受惠遠東新與裕民轉投資支
撐，彌補本業缺口，其中權益法投資認列13.95 億元，金融資
產評價利益 26.58 億元。單季稅後獲利跳增至 52.27 億元，
QoQ 176.98%，YoY 78.40%，EPS 達 1.57 元。

第一金證券投資顧問股份有限公司(First Capital Management Inc.) 台北市長安東路一段 35 號 3F   TEL(02) 2563 -1666 
營業執照字號:114 金管投顧新字第 013 號 
2 
 ⚫ 本業展望保守，惟業外貢獻支撐配息下檔保護 
展望後市，隨中國房市未見起色，台灣建築市場仍以存量消耗
為主，亞泥下半年整體營運將延續本業築底、轉投資彌補整體
獲利之基調。預期公司全年 EPS 為 3.27 元，按照過往 65%
配息率推估，預估 2027 可配發 2.13 元現金股利。

水泥：中國 L 型築底，台灣靜待房市鬆綁 
中國市場受房地產需求量下行與市場報價維持低位，需求呈L
型復甦。亞泥中國採取保市佔策略，預估全年出貨量約下滑
2%， 幅度低於中國平均約10%衰退 。 展望下半年 ， 預期26Q3
報價持平，但受煤炭成本維持高位盤整，預期26Q3 噸毛利恐
略低於 26Q2，全年噸毛利預估落於 15 元人民幣附近。台灣
市場上半年受房市信用管制及土方新制干擾，需求量下滑約
15%。然台灣水泥報價維持平穩，且預拌混凝土事業受惠於科
技廠辦需求支撐，毛利率表現相對穩健。下半年隨政策底部逐
漸浮現與剛性買盤進場，後續將持續觀察開工與施工進度。

資料來源:亞洲水泥 26Q2 法說、第一金投顧匯整

非水泥事業：嘉惠具基本盤，不鏽鋼維持損平 
嘉惠電力下半年營運趨勢預期與 26Q2 相當 ， 主要受國際天然
氣價格走揚使成本承壓，預期營利呈小幅年減。但受惠與台電
的保證發電量合約機制，嘉惠仍具獲利下檔支撐，後續成長空
間端視氣價與台電調度、增購需求而定。遠龍不鏽鋼事業則因
終端市況偏弱，加上海外原料採購及庫存調節具波動性，預期
下半年獲利將維持於損益兩平線波動。 
業外貢獻支撐，預估 2026 全年 EPS 為 3.27 元 
綜上所述，儘管短期兩岸水泥本業面臨逆風，但業外貢獻仍對
合併獲利提供支撐，研究部預估 2026 亞泥全年營收 673.20
億，YoY -5.23%；毛利率 13.67%，年減 2.16 個百分點；
營業利益 61.14 億，YoY -27.27%； 稅後獲利 111.42 億，YoY 
11.09%；EPS 為 3.27 元；配息後每股淨值約為 51.02 元。

雖受中國煤炭成本攀升及報價低迷影響，拖累合併毛利率降至
14.03%；然受惠遠東新與裕民等業外轉投資強勢挹注，單季 EPS
仍逆勢跳增至 1.57 元 。 展望後市 ， 考量中國水泥市場呈L 型築底且
台灣靜待房市鬆綁 ， 本業延續承壓基調 。 研究部預估2026 全年 EPS
為 3.27 元；若以過往 65%配息率推估，2027 年有望配發 2.13 元
現金股利，配息後每股淨值約為 51.02 元。
評價方面，考量整體水泥產業仍處於下行階段，且本業營收與獲利
持續承壓 ， 給予過往區間中緣偏下之0.7 倍 PBR 評價 ， 目標價設定
為 37 元。惟業外獲利強勁有效彌補本業缺口，並提供配息下檔保

護；鑑於公司具備長期殖利率保護優勢，投資建議維持區間操作，
建議可逢低布局。

## Document 005: 1104環泥 宏遠

Source PDF: all report pdf/1104環泥 宏遠.pdf
Report source: line報告備份
Date: 2026-08-19

### Page 1

Company Report 
公司研究報告 
本報告僅供宏遠投顧內部及客戶參考，雖已力求正確與完整，但因時間及市場客觀因素改變所造成產業、市場
或個股之相關條件改變，投資人需自行考量投資之實際狀況與風險，本公司恕不負擔任何法律責任及做任何保
證。非經本公司同意，不得將本報告加以引用或轉載 
1 
1104 環球水泥 ( UCC )

報告日期： 2026 / 08 / 18 研 究 員：林奕頎 (Yichi Lin)  
壹、投 資 建 議 
一.公告 2Q / 202 6 稅後 EPS + 0.71 元， 本業衰退，依賴業外挹注  
環泥 公告 2Q / 2026 財報 單季 營收 YoY – 9%，即使環泥已經改
採 進 口 水 泥 ， 降 低 混 凝 土 成 本 ， 但2Q / 2026 單 季 營 利 率 + 
14.5% ，仍低於 2Q / 2025 同期的  + 15.2% ，2Q/ 2026 單季營業利
益 YoY – 13% ； 依賴 2Q / 2026 認列較高額股利收入 （過去年
度，多數股利認在 3Q，2Q / 2026 則不同） ，拉高業外收淨收支； 
此外，2Q / 2026 單季稅率  9.0%，低於 2Q / 2025 同期的  13.4% ； 
因此， 2Q / 2026 單季稅後 EPS + 0.71 元， YoY + 25% ，高於 2Q / 
2025 同期的  + 0.57 元。  
二.營收 衰退， 凸顯產業困境，長期下滑趨勢日益明顯  
環泥 2Q / 2026 單季  三大營收 YoY 都明顯下滑：  混凝土營收
YoY  - 18%（連續 2 季下滑） ；  水泥 YoY  - 17%；（連續 7 季下
滑） ；  石膏板 YoY - 3%（連續 5 季下滑） 。 環泥三大營收下
滑，凸顯  台灣不動產景氣下滑  及 台商建廠 潮退燒  的困境。
未來，產業 的長期下滑趨勢 ，恐將日益明顯。  
三.環泥 改採進口水泥 ； 中南部  殺價競爭  漸趨激烈  
環泥 2025 年 11 月起，全面 改採較便宜的進口水泥作為混凝土
原料， 降低混凝土成本 。 長期來看，將加速水泥混凝土整體產
業獲利 向下滑落；  台灣的水泥及混凝土產能都明顯過剩 ，主要
是透過 業者自律 ，避免嚴重價格競爭，維持較佳獲利；  目前 ，
環泥及國產 改採進口水泥 ，台泥及亞泥 只能 進行價格戰， 以搶
回市場佔有率， 最終會將價格 水準 壓到讓使用進口水泥的混凝
土業者無利可圖 。  依據新聞，台灣區預拌混凝土公會指出，
2025 年台灣預拌混凝土總銷售量  4,551 萬立方米；  估 2026 年
將跌破  4,000 萬立方米， YoY 下滑超過  1 成； 目前  台南、高
雄、台中、彰化  等 中南部地區，已經出現價格競爭 ，價格下
降 10% ~ 15% ； 凸顯  進口水泥增加、國內建案開工率下降、
高科技建廠高 峰結束 等 利空，衝擊轉 強。   環 泥 的市場 偏重
南部，已成殺價重心地區， 過去依賴業者自律的協同模式逐漸
被打破，回歸市場競爭，長期的殺價 競爭 恐將成為常態 。 
四.建議： 產業競爭日益激烈，本 業獲利下滑趨勢明顯 ；展望保守  
環泥 2Q/2026 財報， 顯示 三大 本業的下滑趨勢，日益明顯 ； 展
望未來，建案開工量減少、台商建廠 潮退燒、 進口水泥 競爭 ，
將使  水泥  及 混凝土  殺價競爭  日益激烈 ； 宏遠認為， 環泥
獲利 處在 長期下滑 趨勢 ； 長期 展望保守 。
投資評等：區間操作 
操作區間：23 ~ 28 元 
  10 ~ 12 X 2026 年稅後 EPS

參考價位：26.75 元 
  P/E：11.3 X 2026 年稅後 EPS 
  P/E：13.8 X 2027 年稅後 EPS 
  2026 / 08 / 17 收盤

混凝土營收：連 2 季 YoY 下滑 
水泥  營收：連 7 季 YoY 下滑

貳、公 司 概 況

他 8%。

二.營運概況 及展望  
1.2026 年混凝土營收明顯下滑 ，產業展望保守看待  
環泥 ，2026 年 1H，6 成營收來自於  混凝土收入。

過去三年， 2023 年 ~ 2025 年， 環泥 的混凝土營收，分別為：  
NT$ 50.02 億元、 51.27 億元、 52.94 億元，呈現 逐年小幅成長 。

但進入 2026 年，環泥混凝土營收 （參閱圖 1）： 
1Q / 2026 單季 ：NT$ 10.73 億元， YoY – 19% 。 
2Q / 2026 單季： NT$ 10.70 億元， YoY – 18% 。

2026 年連續 2 季，混凝土營收 YoY 出現明顯下滑；  
同時， 2Q 的混泥土營收  低於  1Q 的農曆過年淡季 ； 
環泥的混凝土營收大減，凸顯產業下行的狀況日益明顯。

由於台灣房地產，成屋過剩日益嚴重，預售案銷售不佳及建案開
工率減少，加上，台 商返鄉建廠潮明顯降溫， 台灣的水泥及混凝
土產業的下行 ，很可能還會持續很長的時間 。

在水泥及混凝土產業下行的狀況下， 進口水泥的大量引進 ， 更讓
產業過剩及同業競爭加重 ， 水泥及混凝土業者獲利很可能 出現 斷
崖式減少 ，成為投資人的風險所在，投資人應提前避開。

違反內容 :
雇主對於機械之原動機、轉軸、齒輪、帶輪、飛輪、傳動輪、傳動帶等有危害勞工之虞之部分，應
有護罩、護圍、套胴、跨橋等設備。;雇主對於使用動力運轉之機械，具有顯著危險者，應於適當位
置設置有明顯標誌之緊急制動裝置，立即遮斷動力並與制動系統連動，能於緊急時快速停止機械之

運轉。;雇主對於機械、設備及其相關配件之掃除、上油、檢查、修理或調整有導致危害勞工之虞者2026/3/12 違反法條 :
勞動基準法第24條。勞動基準法第39條
違反內容 :
休息日出勤未依規定加給工資;假日工資未給或於休假日工作未依規定加給工資

和成 (1810 TT)  Buy

無人機載具出貨台系廠商。  
投資建議 ：軍工國防產品放量出貨，加上本業衛浴系統穩定成
長，預估 2026 年軍工營收比重約 10% 、 2027 年有望提升至逾
15% 。 08/14/2026 立法院正式三讀通過 6 年合計約台幣 442 億
元之無人載具產業發展統籌型計畫預算案，以及行政院各部會
編列之 192 億元採購預算，全力支持台灣國防安全事業。目前
股價處於評價面 PBR 歷史區間 0.4X~1.3X 中間值。給予投資建
議 Buy ，目標價 34 元 (2026 年 PBR 1.18X) 。  
精密陶瓷事業，佈局國防領域，推出 RF3 等級抗彈板 ：積極佈
局國防領域。現階段 RF2 等級抗彈板持續出貨中，通路經銷商
等期望公司加速最高等級 RF3 抗彈板研發並供貨。因供給相對
有限，需求提升，預估該產品毛利表現佳。  
強化碳纖維複合材料，運用於無人機載具： 開發碳纖維複合材
料，主要運用於無人機之機殼與輪圈，且 2025 年已開始出貨。
無人機載具產品，主要出貨給漢翔、雷虎等台系廠商，配合開
發無人機、無人艇外殼材料。和成與雷虎共同開發小型、中型
無人機機身之碳纖維複合材料，正放量出貨中。  
立法院正式通過無人載具 634 億元預算案： 08/14/2026 立法院
正式三讀通過 6 年合計約台幣 442 億元之無人載具產業發展統
籌型計畫預算案，以及行政院各部會編列之 192 億元採購預算，
總金額約 634 億元，展現強化台灣國防安全企圖心。  
2026/2027 年軍工產品挹注，碳纖複合材料持續出貨： 2026 年
預估本業衛浴設備持平 2025 年，增加無人機載具貢獻，預估軍
工營收比重約 10%，2027 年預期本業有望較 2026 年微幅成長，
且 2027 年軍工營收比重有望提升至逾 15% 。研究部預估 2026
年營收 46.83 億元， YoY+2.76% ，稅後純益 12.44 億元，
YoY+8,597.62% ，稅後 EPS 4.12 元； 2027 年營收 52.36 億元，
YoY+11.82% ，稅後純益 2.74 億元， YoY-77.98% ，稅後 EPS 0.91

2 群益投顧 
  和成 ESG ：  
和成 HCG 集團於 08/12/2025 上傳申報並公告 2024 年度 ESG 永續報告書。
永續經營方面 ， 針對溫室氣體報告以 2024 年度為基準年自主揭露營運概況 ，
以降低溫室氣體排放為目標，汰換柴 油堆高機為電動堆高機，預計 2027 年
底前更新柴油設備 4 台為電動堆高機，減少碳排放量約 18 公噸 /年。以善盡
企業社會責任為己任，榮獲第 8 屆台灣陶藝金陶獎，並舉辦后里森林復育計
畫。節水衛浴系統方面，取得 2025 年金點新秀設計獎，並馳援花蓮馬太鞍
溪堰塞湖災害援助，提供馬桶、臉盆及水龍頭等衛浴設備， 2.5~6 折優惠；
品牌永續方面，旗下包含和成三廠，以及台北、台中、台南、高雄等 4 大展
示中心，並於馬尼拉設立旗艦展示中心。  
公司簡介：  
和成成立於 12/09/1961 ，並於 10/14/1991 掛牌上市。主要產品包括各種衛浴
設備及周邊產品生產銷售，並積極佈局精密陶瓷事業、強化碳纖複合材料等
新材料事業。生產基地有桃園、新北、中國江蘇及菲律賓馬尼拉。  
2026 年超配現金股利：  
經董事會通過，和成 2026 年配發 2025 年度每股現金股利 0.2 元，現金配發
率達 400% 。  
表一、和成近 10 年 (2016~2025) 股利政策

持續推出新型衛浴產品，主要致力於開發 UB 整體衛浴 -模塊裝配式衛浴，
公司主要負責安裝防水盤、壁板及天花板、衛浴設備、燈具、排氣扇等產品。

桶及精湛型超級馬桶、薄型系列龍頭。

3 群益投顧 
級抗彈板持續出貨中， RF3 等級抗彈板於會展後詢問度大幅上揚，通路經銷
商等期望公司加速最高等級 RF3 抗彈板研發並供貨。因供給相對有限，需
求提升，預估該產品毛利表現佳。  
圖二、和成抗彈板

資料來源：和成法說簡報  
強化碳纖維複合材料，運用於無人機載具：  
和成除持續深耕本業衛浴系統外，為強化轉型動能，積極開發碳纖維複合材
料，主要運用於無人機之機殼與輪圈， 2023 年陸續生產，且 2025 年已開始
出貨。無人機載具產品，主要出貨給漢翔、雷虎等台系廠商，配合開發無人
機、無人艇外殼材料。和成與雷虎共同開發小型、中型無人機機身之碳纖維
複合材料，正放量出貨中，且和成亦提供高純度氧化鋁、碳化矽等陶瓷產品，
配合雷虎生產無人艇引擎之遮蔽熱源及抗雷達偵測設備，且處於測試過程。
為打入半導體擴建廠房商機，亦提供無塵室格子樑及鋪設於店面之導管，以
SMC/ 熱壓成型製程製造。受惠日本大廠 TOTO 打入半導體供應鏈，可望為
具備精密陶瓷及高溫燒結技術，身兼半導體級製程關鍵材料技術之和成營
運添磚加瓦，可運用於半導體產業與國防裝備上，增添打入半導體設備關鍵
零組件供應鏈想像。

### Page 4

個股報告  
                                        2026 年 8 月 17 日

4 群益投顧 
立法院正式通過無人載具 634 億元預算案：  
08/14/2026 立法院正式三讀通過 6 年合計約台幣 442 億元之無人載具產業
發展統籌型計畫預算案，以及行政院各部會編列之 192 億元採購預算，總金
額約 634 億元，展現強化台灣國防安全企圖心。外貿協會董事長更表示無人
機產業馬上要起飛，且將持續協助相關業者開拓海外營運版圖。無人機事業
相關供應商，包括雷虎、漢翔、中光電、長榮航太等，而和成主要提供上述
廠商無人機、無人艇之碳纖維複合材料。  
1Q26 認列搬遷補償利益 14.33 億元，單季獲利超越 2025 年：  
和成 1Q26 營收 10.35 億元， QoQ-7.74% 、YoY-8.14% ；營費毛利 1.80 億元，
毛利率約 17.36% ；業外收支 14.13 億元，主因認列蘇州潤淞開發建設之搬
遷補償利益 14.33 億元、租金股利及權利金等收入 1,850 萬元，以及其他利
益與損失 4,198.7 萬元；稅後純益 10.98 億元， QoQ+31,877.38% 、 YoY 虧轉
盈，稅後 EPS 3.63 元。  
2Q26 受惠軍工產品出貨增加，毛利率大幅提升至 25.40% ：  
和成 2Q26 營收 11.47 億元， QoQ+10.81% 、 YoY-8.28% ；營費毛利 2.91 億
元，毛利率約 25.40% ，主因軍工產品出貨量增加，營收比重同步提升；惟
業外收支因 1Q26 基期較高，大幅下滑，使得稅後虧損 6 百萬元， QoQ 盈轉
虧、 YoY 盈轉虧，稅後 EPS -0.02 元。  
2026/2027 年軍工產品挹注，碳纖複合材料持續出貨：  
2026 年主要營運動能，包括衛浴本業、無人機載具及半導體晶圓載具、軌
道工程產品開發等。預估本業衛浴設備持平 2025 年，將持續強化整體衛浴
UB 產品，增加無人機載具貢獻，預估軍工營收比重約 10%。2027 年受惠營
建產業可望落底逐步回升，預期本業有望較 2026 年微幅成長，且 2027 年軍
工產品營收比重有望提升至逾 15% 。研究部預估 2026 年營收 46.83 億元，
YoY+2.76% ，稅後純益 12.44 億元， YoY+8,597.62% ，稅後 EPS 4.12 元；
2027 年營收 52.36 億元， YoY+11.82% ，稅後純益 2.74 億元， YoY-77.98% ，
稅後 EPS 0.91 元。  
投資建議：  
軍工國防產品放量出貨，加上本業衛 浴系統穩定成長，預估 2026 年軍工營
收比重約 10% 、 2027 年有望提升至逾 15% 。 08/14/2026 立法院正式三讀通
過 6 年合計約台幣 442 億元之無人載具產業發展統籌型計畫預算案，以及
行政院各部會編列之 192 億元採購預算，全力支持台灣國防安全事業。目前
股價處於評價面 PBR 歷史區間 0.4X~1.3X 中間值。給予投資建議 Buy ，目
標價 34 元 (2026 年 PBR1.18X) 。

  評等  定義  
  強力買進 (Strong Buy)  首次評等潛在上漲空間 ≥ 35%  
  買進 (Buy)  15% ≤首次評等潛在上漲空間 <35%  
  區間操作 (Trading Buy) 5% ≤首次評等潛在上漲空間 <15%

中立 (Neutral)  
無法由基本面給予投資評等  
預期近期股價將處於盤整  
建議降低持股

【投資評等調整機制】  
  投資評等係「首次」給予特定個股投資評等時，其前一日收盤價相較 3 個月
目標價之潛在上漲空間計算而得。個股投資評等分為四個等級，定義如上。
爾後的投資評等係依循「首次評等」，直到停止推薦。   
「強力買進」、「買進」及「區間操作」均有 upside 目標價。差別僅在於，
首次評等時潛在報酬率不同。「中立」則無目標價。  
一旦我們給予特定個股「強力買進」 、 「買進」或「區間操作」之投資評等，
就是責任的開始，爾後將透過各式研究報告作定期性、持續性追蹤基本面及
股價變化，直到停止推薦。  
停止推薦情境：  
1.  達目標價。  
2.  雖未達原訂目標價，但檢視基本面、訊息面、籌碼面等多方訊息，研
判股價上漲空間已然有限，將適時出具降評報告。  
3.  推薦後股價不漲反跌，亦將出具降評報告。

【免責聲明】  
  本研究報告僅提供予特定人之客戶作為參考資料「非經同意不得轉載」 。我
們並不確保此資訊的完整性與正確性，投資人應了解，報告中有關未來預測
之陳述可能不會實現，因而不應被依賴。而且此報告並非根據特定投資目的
或依預定對象之財務狀況所撰寫出來的，因此，此研究報告的目的，既非對
投資人於買賣證券、選擇權、期貨或其他證券相關之衍生性商品提供詢價服
務，亦非作為進行交易的要約。投資人應注意到相關證券之價值及收益，可
能會有無預警地上升或下降，產生投資回報金額可能比原始投資來得少的
情形。

Applied Materials, Inc. (AMAT-US)  
財報 FIRST TAKE — AI 產能大基建的最大贏家，財測超標但「不夠驚喜」

個股基本資料： 
Applied Materials, Inc. (AMAT-US)

資料來源：Factset，Bloomberg 
 評論及分析 (Overall Summary) 
Applied Materials, Inc. (AMAT) 2026 財年第三季 (Q3 FY2026) 財報展現公司
受惠於全球 AI 基礎設施的快速建設與對先進晶片需求激增的強勁動能。憑藉
在 AI 運算相關高效能與高價值技術領域的領導地位，公司實現創紀錄的單季
營收與獲利，並創下歷史最高的季增長率。在半導體系統與全球服務業務  
(AGS) 雙引擎發力推動下，單季營收達到 91 億美元。儘管面臨建廠無塵室空
間受限與短期擴產的人力成本挑戰，但客戶為確保供應鏈穩定，積極提供長
達八個季度的滾動預測，針對技術藍圖的能見度甚至延伸至 2030 年。隨著業
界迅速朝向先進邏輯製程、 DRAM 及先進封裝  (Advanced Packaging) 發
展，Applied Materials 透過其深厚的材料工程技術與 EPIC 創新平台，已進一
步鞏固其市場領導地位。展望 2026 財年第四季，在 DRAM 強勁復甦與領先
晶圓代工邏輯製程推動下，公司預期營收將再次大幅跳升，持續作為 AI 發展
趨勢下的主要受惠者。 
公司財報重點數據： 
Applied Materials 在 2026 財年第三季展現強勁的財務表現，單季總營收達 
91 億美元，不僅季增 15%，年增更達 25%，創下歷史新高。受惠於價值定價 
(Value-based pric ing) 策略及高利潤產品組合， Non-GAAP 毛利率微升至  
50.4%，較上季成長  40 個基點，較去年同期增加  150 個基點；同時  Non-
GAAP 營業利益率也擴張至 34% 的歷史新高，年增 330 個基點。亮眼的營運
表現帶動第三季 Non-GAAP 每股盈餘 (EPS) 達 3.50 美元（+41%YoY）。 
公司業績表現： 
⚫ 半導體系統 (Semiconductor Systems) 業務：營收達創紀錄的 70 億美元 
(+27%YoY)。其中，DRAM 營收 (包含 HBM 封裝) 較去年同期增長 52% 
創下新高。在沉積 (包含 PVD、CVD 及磊晶)、材料改質、材料移除 (蝕刻
與 CMP) 以及製程診斷與控制 (PDC) 等多個領域皆創下營收紀錄。該部門 
Non-GAAP 毛利率升至 55.4%，Non-GAAP 營業利益達 27 億美元。 
⚫ 全球服務 (Applied Global Services, AGS) 業務：營收達 18 億美元 (年增 
22%)。受惠於訂閱服務成長與高交易性零件需求，並利用  AI 與倉儲自動
化提升營運效率，部門毛利率達 35.6%，營業利益率達 30.1%。目前全球
已有超過 37,000 個機台連接至其專屬的 AIx 軟體平台。 
⚫ 其他業務 (Other)：營收約 2.94 億美元，主要為顯示器 (Display) 業務。

複委託報告 August 14, 2026

### Page 2

2
公司展望與產業前景：
⚫ 2026 財政年度第四季指引 ：預估營收將落在  102.5 億美元區間  (+
51%YoY)。半導體系統營收預計約 79 億美元 (+62%YoY)，AGS 營收約
18.4 億美元  (年增  22%)。Non-GAAP 毛利率預估約  50.4%，Non-
GAAP EPS 預計為 4.02 美元 (+85%YoY)。
⚫ 先進封裝與 PDC 前景：預期 2026 日曆年的整體先進封裝營收將成長超
過 70%，而製程診斷與控制 (PDC) 業務在今年也將成長超過 50%。
⚫ 產能擴張與中國市場 ：為了應對強勁的市場需求，公司本季新增超過
1,500 名製造與客服人員，並計畫在 2028 年前將系統產出能力翻倍。中
國市場本季佔半導體系統與 AGS 總營收的 26%，在 28 奈米代工邏輯投
資推動下，預期今年將持續成長。
電話會議 Q&A 重點摘要：
⚫ 訂單能見度與長期合約 ：管理層指出，針對先進晶片供不應求的情況，
大型客戶提供了長達八個季度的詳細滾動式預測，部分關鍵客戶針對技

術藍圖的能見度更延伸至  2030 年。這種高能見度顯著改善了公司與供
應鏈的產能規劃。
⚫ 毛利率與定價策略動態 ：分析師關注毛利率在營收大幅增長下於下一季
度的指引為何持平。管理層解釋，短期內因大量招募工程師與服務人員
產生了「爬坡成本」(Ramp costs) 抵銷了部分規模經濟優勢。然而，透
過「價值定價」(Value-based pricing) 策略，半導體系統毛利率已穩居
55% 以上，長期來看整體毛利率仍將保持增長趨勢。
⚫ ICAPS 與各終端市場復甦：針對 ICAPS ( 物聯網、通訊、汽車、電源與
感測器) 市場，公司預期該領域的設備消化期已接近尾聲，隨著客戶端
產能利用率上升以及電源、光子晶片的亮點需求，預期 ICAPS 整體在今
年及明年皆將重返成長軌道。
公司簡介：
Applied Materials, Inc. 是全球領先的半導體與顯示器設備及材料工程解決方
案供應商。該公司致力於推動資料中心、人工智慧  (AI) 運算基礎架構與先進
應用的底層晶片製造技術。其廣泛的產品線涵蓋沉積、材料移除、先進封裝

至製程診斷控制系統 (PDC)，並透過 Applied Global Services (AGS) 提供高
度依賴  AI 預 測 分 析 的 機 台 最 佳 化 解 決 方 案 。 近 期 更 透 過 設 立 EPIC
(Equipment and Process Innovation and Commercialization) 中心，進一
步深化與頂尖晶片製造商及研究機構的早期共同創新 (Co-innovation)。
競爭對手與公司自身價值：
Applied Materials 在全球半導體製造設備市場中面臨眾多同業的競爭。相較
於競爭對手， AMAT 的核心價值與護城河在於其最廣泛且高度互補的技術組
合 (特別在先進邏輯製程、DRAM 及先進封裝)。該公司透過全新的 EPIC 平台
讓客戶在架構發展的極早期階段便能接觸最新設備創新，從而加速新技術導
入並提升晶圓廠的生產良率與單位面積產出。這種深度的技術整合與協助客
戶提升廠區投資報酬率的能力，使其在 AI 軍備競賽中擁有無可取代的產業地

位。

Asia Vital Components

Buy: 2Q26 margins another record highs; 
solid 2H26 guidance; lift PO to NT$3,888

Reiterate Rating: BUY | PO: 3,888 TWD | Price: 2,910 TWD

2Q26 beat, with margins at another quarterly record high 
Asia Vital Components (AVC) held 2Q26 physical and virtual meetings on 12 Aug. All its 
margins hit another quarterly record high, with GM/OPM of 32.6%/27.4%, above BofAe’s 
30.0%/24.2% and consensus’ 31.0%/25.2%. OP (+12% QoQ, +165% YoY) was 11%/7% 
above BofAe/consensus, despite sales (flat QoQ, +66% YoY) missing BofAe/consensus 
both by 2%, dragged by supply-chain component hiccup. We attribute the beat mainly to 
stronger server margins and stringent cost control. Server/networking accounted for 
66% of 2Q26 sales, vs 66%/51% in 1Q26/2Q25. With a non-operating gain of 
NT$457mn, mainly from net interest income, NP was 18%/11% above consensus.  
Better 2H vs 1H; new projects mass production in 3Q-4Q 
Mgmt. expects QoQ sales growth in 3Q26 and better 2H26 vs 1H26, amid more server 
projects entering mass production. Our supply-chain checks suggest mass production for 
Nvidia Vera Rubin GPU server project will start from 3Q26 (Aug/Sep), and liquid-cooled 
ASIC (AWS Trainium 3) project in 4Q26. We now expect 3Q/4Q26 sales to rise 16%/19% 
QoQ and 47%/42% YoY. Into 2027, mgmt. sees rising orders/volume from GPU and ASIC 
server projects, and still expects ASIC sales mix to surpass GPU server mix. Mgmt. 
maintained 2026 capex guidance at NT$15bn and sees an even higher level in 2027 
(BofAe: NT$17bn), mainly for capacity expansion in Vietnam for liquid-cooling products 
(eg. cold plate, rack/inner manifolds and CDUs).  
Buy: lift PO to NT$3,888; still our top cooling pick 
We lift 2026-28E EPS by 7-11%, mainly to reflect 2Q26 beat and higher GM/OPM 
forecasts, supported by the solid AI server business and a favorable mix. We lift our PO 
to NT$3,888 from NT$3,650, with the valuation methodology unchanged at 27x 2027E 
EPS. We reiterate Buy on AVC as our top pick in the cooling sub-sector, which should 
benefit from robust GPU/ASIC AI server demand and rising liquid-cooling adoption. We 
also like its broad product/project/client base to drive solid sales and margin growth.

Company Sector 
IT Hardware

Company Description 
AVC is the leading system-level thermal/cooling component 
maker globally. Key end markets include server, PC, 
smartphone. Key end clients include global CSPs, PC brands, 
smartphone brands. AVC was founded in 1991 and went 
publicly listed on Taiwan Stock Exchange on 27 Sep 2002. It 
is headquartered in Taiwan. Its main production sites are 
located in China (Shenzhen, Wuhan, Shanghai, Dongguan, 
Chengdu), with ongoing capacity expansion in Vietnam.

Investment Rationale 
We assign a Buy rating to AVC given its leading position in 
thermal/cooling component and total solution strategy to 
ride on strong AI market demand, double-digit sales/EPS 
CAGR in 2026-28 with rising liquid cooling sales mix and 
ASP/margin increase.

### Page 6

Price objective basis & risk 
Asia Vital Components (AVMPF) 
Our PO of NT$3,888 is based on 27x 2027E EPS. We use 27x P/E multiple (3SD above 
P/E average since 2020) in view of AVC's double-digit sales/EPS CAGR in 2026-28E. We 
believe AVC's leading position in thermal/cooling component market and strong AI 
market demand will drive rising liquid cooling sales mix and ASP/margin increase.

Upside risks to our PO are: (1) stronger demand in AI server, server, PC, smartphone, (2) 
better operating leverage with larger revenue scale, (3) less intensive competition, (4) 
faster product certification, (5) faster capacity expansion, (6) favorable FX movement 
(USD/RMB and/or USD/NTD) to help on GM and/or non-operating items.

Downside risks to our PO are: (1) softer AI server demand due to GPU/component 
shortage, (2) weaker server/PC/smartphone market demand amid inflation/macro 
uncertainties, (3) GM pressure due to higher material/labor/utility cost, (4) more 
intensive competition, (5) delayed product certification, (6) slower capacity expansion, 
(7) unfavorable FX movement (USD/RMB and/or USD/NTD) to weigh on GM and/or non-
operating items.

R1 Issuers that were investment banking clients of BofA Securities or one of its affiliates within the past 12 months. For purposes of this Investment Rating Distribution, the coverage universe includes only stocks. A stock 
rated Neutral is included as a Hold, and a stock rated Underperform is included as a Sell.       FUNDAMENTAL EQUITY OPINION KEY: Opinions include a Volatility Risk Rating, an Investment Rating and an Income Rating. VOLATILITY RISK RATINGS, indicators 
of potential price fluctuation, are: A - Low, B - Medium and C - High. INVESTMENT RATINGS reflect the analyst’s assessment of both a stock’s absolute total return 
potential as well as its attractiveness for investment relative to other stocks within its Coverage Cluster (defined below). Our investment ratings ar e: 1 - Buy stocks 
are expected to have a total return of at least 10% and are the most attractive stocks in the coverage cluster; 2 - Neutral stocks are expected to remain flat or 
increase in value and are less attractive than Buy rated stocks and 3 - Underperform stocks are the least attractive stocks in a coverage cluster. An investment 
rating of 6 (No Rating) indicates that a stock is no longer trading on the basis of fundamentals. Analysts assign investment ratings considering, among other things, 
the 0-12 month total return expectation for a stock and the firm’s guidelines for ratings dispersions (shown in the table below). The current price objective for a 
stock should be referenced to better understand the total return expectation at any given time. The price objective reflects the analyst’s view of the potential price 
appreciation (depreciation). 
Investment rating Total return expectation (within 12-month period of date of initial 
rating) 
Ratings dispersion guidelines for coverage clusterR2 
Buy ≥ 10% ≤ 70% 
Neutral ≥ 0% ≤ 30% 
Underperform N/A ≥ 20% 
R2Ratings dispersions may vary from time to time where BofA Global Research believes it better reflects the investment prospects of stocks in a Coverage Cluster. 
INCOME RATINGS, indicators of potential cash dividends, are: 7 - same/higher (dividend considered to be secure), 8 - same/lower (dividend not considered to be 
secure) and 9 - pays no cash dividend.  Coverage Cluster is comprised of stocks covered by a single analyst or two or more analysts sharing a common industry, 
sector, region or other classification(s). A stock’ s coverage cluster is included in the most recent BofA Global Research report referencing the stock.

### Page 6

Lotes  (3533.TW)
14 August 2026 Citi Research
6
Lotes
Company description
Established in 1986 and listed in 2007, Lotes is a leading solution provider of
connectors and cable assembly services based in Keelung, Taiwan. The
company runs a fully integrated business model, with product design, tooling,
molding, stamping, electro-plating and automation process all under the
same roof. Majority of Lotes’ manufacturing capacity is located in China,
Guangzhou, Huanan, and Suzhou, with headquarters located in Keelung,

Taiwan. The main focus areas include CPU socket/ILM and connectors for
DDR, PCIe, SATA, Type-C, and etc. Specifically for CPU socket, Lotes is now
the No.2 supplier globally with 25%+ of global market share. Lotes also owns
3,200+ patents globally for product design and manufacturing process
protection, which has raised the bar for new competition. Lotes started the
business as a PC connector supplier but has shifted its focus to server and
other non-PC business in recent years. Management targets to increase its

non-PC related exposure 70% to total sales in next 5 years, vs 56% in 2018.
Investment strategy
We rate Lotes as Buy. Lotes has c25%+ global market share in CPU sockets
and is seeing rising exposure to servers. We expect Lotes to grow its sales and
earnings at 16% and 27% CAGR in 2023-25E, backed by server sales growth,
CPU platform migration and spec migration in sockets. We forecast server-
related sales to account for 30-35% of total sales in 2024-25E, vs. 28% in

2023. As server products generate better margins than PCs, we expect
margins to improve based on product mix changes and better manufacturing
yield as well as cost efficiency. We also expect CPU platform transition and
spec migration of connectors to lead to ASP increases amid limited
competition.
Valuation
Our target price for Lotes is NT$2,500. We value Lotes based on PE as we
believe the company’s earnings performance and industry dynamics would be

the key focus of the market. Our target price is based on 20x 2027E EPS,
which is at the higher-end of the historical trading range since 2015 and is
justified by the company’s successful transformation from a PC-centric
component maker to a company with increasing server exposure (posing
higher entry barriers), robust earnings growth and margin expansion, as well
as rising ROE.
Risks
Key downside risks that could cause Lotes shares to trade below our target

price include: 1) Potential delays in CPU launch schedule; 2) Peer competition;
3) Raw material price hike; 4) Patent litigation; and 5) FX volatility.
Prepared for Kevin Lu

Guide to Citi Research Fundamental Research Investment Ratings:
Citi Research stock recommendations include an investment rating and an optional risk rating to highlight high risk stocks.

Risk rating takes into account both price volatility and fundamental criteria. Stocks will either have no risk rating or a High risk
Prepared for Kevin Lu

### Page 9

Lotes  (3533.TW)
14 August 2026 Citi Research
9
rating assigned.
Investment Ratings: Citi Research investment ratings are Buy, Neutral and Sell. Our ratings are a function of analyst
expectations of expected total return ("ETR") and risk. ETR is the sum of the forecast price appreciation (or depreciation) plus
the dividend yield for a stock within the next 12 months.  The target price is based on a 12 month time horizon. The Investment
rating definitions are: Buy (1) ETR of 15% or more or 25% or more for High risk stocks; and Sell (3) for negative ETR. Any covered

stock not assigned a Buy or a Sell is a Neutral (2). For stocks rated Neutral (2), if an analyst believes that there are insufficient
valuation drivers and/or investment catalysts to derive a positive or negative investment view, they may elect with the approval
of Citi Research management not to assign a target price and, thus, not derive an ETR. Citi Research may suspend its rating
and target price and assign “Rating Suspended” status for regulatory and/or internal policy reasons. Citi Research may also

suspend its rating and target price and assign "Under Review" status for other exceptional circumstances (e.g. lack of
information critical to the analyst's thesis, trading suspension) affecting the company and/or trading in the company's
securities. In both such situations, the rating and target price will show as “—“ and “-“ respectively in the rating history price
chart. Prior to 11 April 2022 Citi Research assigned “Under Review” status to both situations and prior to 11 Nov 2020 only in

exceptional circumstances. As soon as practically possible, the analyst will publish a note re-establishing a rating and
investment thesis. Investment ratings are determined by the ranges described above at the time of initiation of coverage, a
change in investment and/or risk rating, or a change in target price (subject to limited management discretion). At times, the
expected total returns may fall outside of these ranges because of market price movements and/or other short-term volatility

or trading patterns. Such interim deviations will be permitted but will become subject to review by Research Management.
Your decision to buy or sell a security should be based upon your personal investment objectives and should be made only
after evaluating the stock's expected performance and risk.
Catalyst Watch/Short Term Views (“STV”) Ratings Disclosure:
Catalyst Watch and STV Upside/Downside calls: Citi Research may also include a Catalyst Watch or STV Upside or Downside

call to indicate the analyst expects the share price to rise (fall) in absolute terms over a specified period of 30 or 90 days in
reaction to one or more specific near-term catalysts or events impacting the company or the market. A Catalyst Watch will be
published when Analyst confidence is high that an impact to share price will occur; it will be a STV when confidence level is
moderate. A Catalyst Watch or STV Upside/Downside call will automatically expire at the end of the specified 30/90 day

period. The Catalyst Watch will also be automatically removed if share price performance (calculated at market close) exceeds
15% against the direction of the call (unless over-ridden by the analyst). The analyst may also remove a Catalyst Watch or STV
call prior to the end of the specified period in a published research note. A Catalyst Watch/STV Upside or Downside call may be
different from and does not affect a stock’s fundamental equity rating, which reflects a longer-term total absolute return

## Document 122: 260813_ms_compal

### Page 1

M
Update
Compal Electronics  | Asia Pacific
2Q26 GM missed despite non-
PC strength; stay UW
PCs are the near-term drag: Management expects its 3Q NB shipment units to be
flat q/q thanks to share gains; higher ASPs support sequential PC revenue growth.
For the full year, Compal expects its PC shipments to decline ~15% y/y, in line with
the market.
AI servers remain the growth driver: AI servers made up ~70% of server revenue in
2Q (servers' share of total revenue was in mid-single digits in 1Q an d high single

digits in 2Q), with new Neocloud wins widening the pipeline. 3Q server revenue is
guided to grow in high double digits q/q. For full-year 2026, servers are targeted at
~10% of the mix (unchanged). They are guided to rise to more than 30–40% in
2027, potentially taking PCs below 50% of total revenue (though we remain
skeptical).
Compal currently mainly ships L6 for general servers and L10 for AI servers (B300
HGX), both of which carry higher GM and OpM vs. AI racks and the corporate

average., but it expects more rack-scale AI server shipments from late 2026
onwards. This implies that Compal's margins could trend lower h/h because of a
different product mix within servers in 2H. Of note,  Compal's AI server business
currently runs 100% on buy-and-sell.

Key Takeaways
CY26 capex is guided to be NT$18bn; 1H26 capex was ~NT$9bn, NT$6-7bn of
which was dedicated to server capacity expansion.

CY27 capex is expected to remain elevated, though no numerical guidance was
provided.
2Q GM was 4.6% (-60bps q/q), 80bps below our estimate/70 bps below the
Street. Higher component costs diluted GM.
2Q OP was NT$2.9bn (+8% q/q), 13% below our estimate/9% below the Street,
for OpM of 1.2% (-10bps q/q), reflecting operating leverage.
2Q non-op income was higher because of FVTPL gains. Thus, NP reached NT
$3.1bn (+59% q/q), 44% above our estimate/39% above the Street, for EPS of NT

$0.72.

Valuation Methodology and Risks
Compal Electronics (2324.TW)
Base case, residual income model. Our key assumptions include a cost of equity of 9.1% (beta
1.3, equity risk premium 6.0%, and risk-free rate 1.5%), a medium-term growth rate of 5.0%,
and a long-term earnings growth rate of 3.0%.
Risks to Upside
n Stronger-than-expected NB and smartphone demand
n Further share gains in iPad
n Margin expansion thanks to better scale

Risks to Downside
n Weaker-than-expected NB demand
n Margin contraction from sales shortfall and fierce price competition
n Weaker-than-expected iPad shipments

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -

Update
Quanta Computer Inc.  | Asia Pacific
2Q26 OpM better than feared;
AI should continue to grow
strongly into CY28
2Q26:
• Servers made up 80%+ of total revenue (within this, AI servers are 75-80%;
ASIC is <10% of AI server mix). NB revenue is a low double-digit portion of
the mix (Chromebooks were <10% of NB mix).
Other takeaways
• CY26 AI servers to grow 100%+ y/y (unchanged); AI servers to reach 80% of
total server revenue (unchanged)
• CY26 general servers to grow y/y (unchanged).

• AI server consignment to start picking up in the mix in 3Q26, but will remain
relatively small.
• CY26 OpM to be similar to C1H26 levels (~3.0%).
• New generation VR200 NVL72 racks will be lower-margin vs. GB300, but
will drive higher profit dollars.
• 3Q NB units down close to 20% q/q.
• CY26 NB units down double-digit y/y.
• CY27 AI testing capacity to double, CY28 AI testing capacity to double again
(implying AI server visibility extending into CY28).

Key Takeaways

q/q.

Quanta continues to win new projects.
Stay OW - valuation looks attractive at ~11x CY27e P/E vs. its three-year historical
range of 6-18x.

Valuation Methodology and Risks
Quanta Computer Inc. (2382.TW)
Base case, residual income model. Key assumptions include a cost of equity of 9.0% (beta of
1.2, equity premium of 6.0% and risk-free rate of 1.5%), an 8.5% medium-term growth rate, and
a 3% terminal growth rate.
Risks to Upside
n Stronger-than-expected NB demand
n Stronger-than-expected Apple Watch demand
n Stronger-than-expected server demand
n Faster-than-expected AI server penetration

Risks to Downside
n Weaker-than-expected NB demand
n Softer-than-expected Apple Watch demand
n Weak margin performance owing to rising labor costs and sales shortfalls
n Fierce price competition in the mega data center segment
n Slower-than-expected AI server penetration

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.

## Document 131: 260814_ml_alchip

Source PDF: all report pdf/260814_ml_alchip.pdf
Report source: line報告備份
Date: 2026-08-14
Pages: 11
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

Alchip

Solid Trn3 ramp at improving production 
margins; Trn4 chipset tape out on track

Reiterate Rating: BUY | PO: 6,100 TWD | Price: 4,210 TWD

Resilient 2Q/2H26 GMs even with strong Trainium3 ramp 
2Q26 sales was +83% QoQ with production business outgrowing NRE driven by initial 
contribution of Trainium3 from June along with healthy China ADAS SoC demand. With 
mix skewed to lower margins production, headcount expansion, annual salary hike and 
more EDA usage, 2Q26 GM/OpM was 35%/22% (vs. 50%/33% in 1Q26), taking 2Q26 
EPS to NT$20.02. While its production business could reach 80-90% of its sales in 
2H26, GM/OpM is guided to stay 20-25%/15% (Trainium1 at 10-15%/10%) on its 
growing value add in back-end design and supply chain support even with COT model. 
Trn3/4 locked in; other CSP CPU/ASIC projects in progress 
We expect Trainium3 to generate US$1.3bn sales to Alchip in ’26 (60% of its total sales) 
and could ramp further in ’27 to US$2.2bn (68% of its sales) before Trainium4 on 2nm 
tapes out in 4Q26 and enters production from 4Q27 for the dollar content uplift on 
higher chiplet design complexity and larger reticle size for the package integrating more 
compute and I/O tiles, along with meaningful volume opportunity as Amazon targets to 
adopt the chipset more aggressively in house and sell Trainium-based hardware noted in 
our preview. On top of the US$4bn revenue in ’28 and US$17bn addressable potential 
(ex-HBM) through the lifecycle for Trainium4, Alchip is engaged with other hyperscalers 
potentially on N5 ARM CPUs and certain part of the ASIC projects for contribution in ‘28. 
Firmer ‘26-28 earnings visibility at compelling valuation 
On ramping Trainium3 revenue and better Trainium4 project visibility supplemented by 
better than feared margins profile for the project from 2H26-2027, we lift ‘26/’27 EPS 
from NT$132.78/NT$201.25 to NT$132.94/NT$204.17. We keep Buy rating and raise 
PO to NT$6,100 (vs. NT$6,000 prior) still on 30x/19x ‘27/28e P/E (vs. its 6-53x range). 
Our analysis shows every 100k incremental Trn3/4 chipset units could add its EPS by 
NT$5/NT$15 in ‘27/’28, putting the share 20x/13x ‘27/28e P/E attractive on the cloud 
ASIC TAM upside.

Company Sector 
Semiconductors

Company Description 
Alchip is a pure IC design service company headquartered in 
Taiwan and has strong presence in Greater China. Its focus 
is on advanced AI/HPC chip-design, largely built on leading 
edge process by the foundry. Beyond Greater China and 
computing chips, it also has customers in US/Europe/Japan, 
etc, and from networking/gaming areas.

Investment Rationale 
We have a Buy rating on Alchip, as we still like its industry 
position for leading edge chip-design, as well as its visibility 
of sustained growth into 2027 along with continuous 
ramps from key projects on mass production side.

### Page 6

Price objective basis & risk 
Alchip (ALCPF) 
Our NT$6,100 PO is based on 30x 2027E P/E, at midcycle of the company's historical 
trading range of 6-53x. In our view, this is backed by 1) operating/net margin of 
18%/17% in 2026-27E, 2) EPS CAGR of 55% during 2026-28E, and 3) ROE of 25-30% in 
2026-28E.

Upside risks: 
1) earlier and faster ramp of other non-AWS projects to enter into production stage 
2) looser restriction from the US on China, where Alchip generates better margin with 
strong demand

Downside risks: 
1) intensified competition pressure from existing and emerging peers, 
2) failure or delay to execute node migration and corresponding product roll-out for 
customers, 
3) a slowdown in foundry node migration, which allows a longer time window for peers 
to catch up in advanced chip-design, or makes AI more easily to replace the chip-design 
service provided by the employees/engineers, 
4) lack of or insufficient supply support (foundry, back-end, etc), especially when demand 
is strong from other customers for these supply chain partners.

Update
King Slide Works Co. Ltd.  | Asia Pacific
Preliminary Profit in July
Announced
King Slide announced its preliminary profit for July, per TWSE regulation:
• Revenue was NT$6,407mn (+44% MoM/+356% YoY), 33% of our estimate at
NT$19,555mn (+81% QoQ/+348% YoY) and 40% of the consensus at NT
$15,883mn (+47% QoQ/+264% YoY) for 3Q26.
• Pretax income was NT$5,985mn (+321% YoY; pretax margin at 93.4%), 37%
of our pretax income estimate at NT$16,377mn (margin at 83.7%) and 45% of

consensus at NT$13,170mn (margin at 82.9%) for 3Q26.
• Net income was NT$4,787mn (+321% YoY; EPS NT$50.24; NPM 74.7%), 37%
of our estimated net income at NT$12,938mn (EPS NT$135.76; NPM 66.2%)
and 46% of consensus at NT$10,460mn (EPS NT$108.72; NPM 65.9%) for
3Q26.
Our view:
• We see upside to our estimates and consensus for 3Q26 earnings.
• There should be some regular interest income and one-off FX gains during
the month, but even with that, our back-of-the-envelop calculation points to

over 85% of operating margin in July vs. 82.1% in 2Q26. We believe this
should strengthen the market's conviction about the sustainability of its
margins in 2H26.
• We maintain our view that King Slide's competitiveness of the
comprehensive patent portfolio and strong execution is entrenched and
accentuated amid the fast generation migration in AI applications.
• Stay OW with PT of NT$15,000 (30x 2027 P/E)

Valuation Methodology and Risks
King Slide Works Co. Ltd. (2059.TW)
Base case, 30x 2027e P/E. We use price/earnings as our valuation methodology because we
believe it better captures King Slide's earnings growth potential. We believe our target mul-
tiple is justified by an operating profit CAGR of 77% seen for 2025-28, implying a PEG of 0.4
vs. the average of 1.2 over 2010-24, when the stock was trading at 17-18x P/E with an operating

profit CAGR of 14%.
Risks to Upside
n Stronger-than-expected AI capex
n Stronger server demand
n Faster market share gains
Risks to Downside
n Softer AI capex
n Weaker server demand
n Sluggish kitchen furniture demand
n Competition leading to slower share gains.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -
relevant MSCI country index or MSCI sub-regional index or MSCI AC Asia Pacific ex Japan Index.

Price Target Risks and Methodology - Auras 
Our 12m target price for Auras is based on a near-term P/E, consistent with our Taiwan 
Technology coverage. We base our 12-m TP of NT$1,353 on a target P/E multiple of 
19.4x forward year EPS (2027E). Our target P/E is derived from the correlation between 
P/E and EPS growth of its peers in technology hardware, based on the company’s 
2027E-28E EPS YoY growth at 23%. Key downside risks: 1) Slower-than-expected liquid 
cooling penetration; (2) Soft AI server demand; (3) Weaker-than-expected PC market; 
and (4) Potential competition from existing and new suppliers.

and represent investment recommendations focused on the size of the total return potential and/or the likelihood of the realization of the return across
their respective areas of coverage.  The addition or removal of stocks from such Conviction Lists are managed by the Investment Review Committee or
other designated committee in each respective region and do not represent a change in the analysts’ investment rating for such stocks.

Total return potential represents the upside or downside differential between the current share price and the price target, including all paid or
anticipated dividends, expected during the time horizon associated with the price target. Price targets are required for all covered stocks. The total
return potential, price target and associated time horizon are stated in each report adding or reiterating an Investment List membership.

## Document 139: 260817_daiwa_Gigabyte

Source PDF: all report pdf/260817_daiwa_Gigabyte.pdf
Report source: line報告備份
Date: 2026-08-17
Pages: 7
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

Taiwan Information Technology

What’s new: We provide our view after 2Q26 results (published 14 August)

What’s the impact: Business outlook. We believe Gigabyte will ride on 
consistently solid AI server demand from neocloud, as key vendors have 
been repeatedly raising their power budget/capex. This tallies with our view 
that neocloud and enterprise will gradually become key buyers of Nvidia AI 
servers from the Vera Rubin generation in 2027. Nebius raised its power 
contract target (by end-2026) from 4GW to 5GW and its management 
believes its longer-term target will be >5GW by 2030 (vs. its current usage 
of c.1GW). Meanwhile, Coreweave raised its 2026 capex guidance to 
USD35-39bn (from USD31-35bn) with an order backlog of USD104bn (vs. 
USD2.6bn revenue in 2Q26). We believe that neocloud will become a key 
computing source for sovereign, enterprise and other corporate customers 
that cannot maintain and design the hardware themselves. As such, the 
TAM for server brands like Dell, Gigabyte, Lenovo and Asustek is likely to 
rise. We raise our revenue assumptions for Gigabyte’s AI server to 
TWD411 in 2026E and TWD565bn in 2027E (from TWD351bn/TWD448bn 
previously and vs. TWD180bn in 2025). We expect the rising revenue 
contribution from AI servers to be margin-dilutive and therefore margins to 
decrease gradually. We project the gross margin to reach 8.9/8.4% in 
2027/28E vs. 12% in 1Q26 (due to a gain from inventory re-evaluation).

2Q26 results beat on sales and FX gain. Gigabyte’s 2Q26 earnings came 
in at TWD6.6bn (EPS: TWD9.9), which was 42% and 19% higher than our 
and consensus estimates, respectively. 2Q26 sales came in at 
TWD144.2bn (up 37% QoQ and 41% YoY), 16% higher than our estimate 
on better-than-expected enterprise AI server demand. However, the gross 
margin decreased 2.4pp QoQ to 9.6%, 0.6pp lower than consensus, mainly 
due to a higher-than-expected contribution from AI server revenue. The 
operating margin also dropped, by 1.5pp QoQ to 5.6%, but this was 
partially offset by the elevated operating leverage on its expanded top line. 
Net profit was boosted by a higher-than-expected FX gain of TWD885m.

What we recommend: We cut our 2027-28E earnings by 2-3% to reflect 
the 2Q26 results and higher AI server contribution as well as the new GDR 
plan that will dilute EPS by c.7%. We reaffirm our Buy (1) rating and raise 
our 12M TP to TWD490 (from TWD422), based on an unchanged PER of 
15x (lower end of its past-1-year trading range of 12-25x) applied to our 1-
year forward diluted EPS. Key downside risk: earnings impact from Nvidia’s 
business model change.

How we differ: Our 2026-28E EPS are 13-22% higher than consensus 
likely on our higher revenue and profitability assumptions.

17 August 2026

2Q26 results: beat on solid operating leverage

➢ Key beneficiary to ride on rising AI demand from neocloud 
➢ Strong execution on opex control due to leverage on rising AI sales 
➢ Reaffirming our Buy (1) rating; raising 12M TP to TWD490

Company profile 
Founded in 1986 and listed on the Taiwan stock exchange in 1998, Gigabyte started as a motherboard 
supplier with its own brand “Gigabyte” and has now become the world's second-largest brand in the 
motherboard business and fourth-largest vendor in the global VGA industry. Its other products include 
graphics cards and servers. The company has also established an e-sports brand called “AORUS” in 
order to enter the gaming market with various products including motherboards, VGA, notebooks, 
desktops, monitors and other PC peripherals. After 20 years of development in the server industry, the 
business finally bore fruit in 2021. As such, Gigabyte spun off its networking business to a 100% owned 
subsidiary “Giga Computing” in Jan. 2023, which enabled Gigabyte to expand the server business in a 
more flexible and efficient way. Gigabyte is currently headquartered in New Taipei City and owns 
production sites in Taoyuan (NB and server), Dongguan (motherboard and VGA), and Ningbo 
(motherboard, VGA and server). The company also owns service centres across all 5 continents.

### Page 2

2Q26 beat: Gigabyte’s 2Q26 revenue was up 41% YoY / 37% QoQ to NT$144bn, or 12% 
/ 9% above our estimate / Bloomberg consensus, which we attribute to the AI server 
business expansion and PC demand pull-in amid memory price hike. GM of 9.6% is lower 
than our estimate and Bloomberg consensus, mainly on the rising shipments of 
rack-level AI server and rising cost of key components (e.g. memory, CPU, PCB, etc). 
2Q26 opex ratio is better than our estimate and Bloomberg consensus, reflecting the 
improving operational efficiency as shipments ramp up. Overall, 2Q26 net income was 
up 117% YoY / 25% QoQ to NT$6.6bn, or 21% higher than our / Bloomberg consensus.

Earnings revision: We factor in Gigabyte’s Jul revenue and 2Q26 result and raise our 
2026-28E net income by 8% / 14% / 13%, mainly on higher revenues. Our 2026-28E 
revenues are revised up by 16% / 22% / 30%, mainly on more AI servers and higher AI 
server racks’ ASP , considering the migration towards next-generation AI server with 
stronger computing power and more complex design. Our GM is lower given the product 
mix toward AI servers. Our OP income raise is smaller than the GP raise given the R&D 
investments to facilitate AI servers technology development. Our NI raise is smaller than 
the OP income raise given the higher interest expense, reflecting the company’s rising 
debt to support the working capital and capex. The company’s operating cash flow is at 
NT$79bn loss in 1H26 (vs. NT$5bn gain in 2025A).

Valuation: We continue to use near-term P/E to derive our 12M TP . Our 2027E target 
P/E is at 14.7x (vs. previously at 16.8x) applied to diluted EPS, still derived from 
correlation of peers’ forward year P/E to avg. EPS YoY (methodology unchanged), 
reflecting the sector de-rating on AI server ODMs amid the model transition and market

concerns on consumer electronics end demand under the high memory costs. With our

Price Target Risks and Methodology - Gigabyte 
We are Neutral rated on Gigabyte with a 12-month target price of NT$487. Our TP is 
based on a 14.7x 2027E P/E which is based on PC/server peers’ EPS growth vs. P/E 
multiple correlation. We see EPS growth as a major factor for the stock’s share 
performance. 
Key upside/downside risks include: 1) stronger-/weaker-than-expected PC market 
recovery, 2) faster-/slower-than-expected gaming PC growth, and 3) 
faster-/slower-than-expected AI server ramp-up.

Ratings, coverage universe and related definitions
Buy (B), Neutral (N), Sell (S) Analysts recommend stocks as Buys or Sells for inclusion on various regional Investment Lists. Being assigned a Buy or Sell
on an Investment List is determined by a stock’s total return potential relative to its coverage universe. Any stock not assigned as a Buy or a Sell on an
Investment List with an active rating (i.e., a  stock that is not Rating Suspended, Not Rated, Early-Stage Biotech, Coverage Suspended or Not Covered), is

deemed Neutral. Each region manages Regional Conviction Lists, which are selected from Buy rated stocks on the respective region’s Investment Lists
and represent investment recommendations focused on the size of the total return potential and/or the likelihood of the realization of the return across
their respective areas of coverage.  The addition or removal of stocks from such Conviction Lists are managed by the Investment Review Committee or
other designated committee in each respective region and do not represent a change in the analysts’ investment rating for such stocks.

Total return potential represents the upside or downside differential between the current share price and the price target, including all paid or
anticipated dividends, expected during the time horizon associated with the price target. Price targets are required for all covered stocks. The total
return potential, price target and associated time horizon are stated in each report adding or reiterating an Investment List membership.

### Page 1

Eclat (1476.TW)
2Q26 Briefing – Constructive 2H Outlook; Easing Input Costs and 
Healthy New Product Momentum
CITI'S TAKE 
We came away from Eclat’s 2Q26 briefing with a constructive view on the 
2H26 demand outlook, supported by six months of order visibility, healthy 
new product development activity, and new customer wins. Raw material 
headwinds should ease sequentially from 3Q as oil prices normalize and 
price negotiation progress. We slightly trim our earnings estimates 2-3% to 
factor in 2Q26 results. We reiterate Buy with an unchanged TP of NT$460 
(17x NTM P/E). With fundamentals turning more positive h/h, we think at 
11.5x NTM P/E, near the low end of its historical range, downside appears 
limited with a 6% dividend yield providing additional valuation support. 
2H26 outlook remains constructive with 6-month visibility — Mgmt. reiterated 
that order visibility remains 6 months and noted that monthly sales of >NT$3bn is 
achievable. Looking further ahead, mgmt. also maintains a cautiously optimistic 
view on 1H27E, indicating that despite potential uncertainties from geo tension and 
macro conditions, product development requests, customer engagement and long-
term order discussion remain active.
New product momentum stays healthy; wellness contribution at ~15% — New 
product development remains one of the more encouraging messages from the 
briefing. Mgmt. highlighted that: 1) wellness products account for ~15% of sales and 
continue to receive good market feedback, 2) brand customers continue to invest in 
product innovation at a healthy pace, 3) new product development and launch ratios 
continue to trend upward, and 4) Eclat is currently developing products with three 
new brand customers. One has already started placing orders while the other two 
remain under development. 
Margin pressure should ease in 3Q; product mix remains the key swing factor — 
2Q GPM declined q/q mainly due to higher raw material costs and an unfavorable 
mix, as stronger-than-expected carry-over product demand diluted the margin 
benefit from new products. Mgmt. expects raw material pressure to ease in 3Q, 
supported by lower oil prices and ongoing customer price negotiations. The pace of 
margin recovery will still depend on the mix between new and carry-over products. 
Mgmt. will continue to target > 30% GPM in 2H26E. (continued)

Earnings estimate revision and TP — We slightly trim our earnings estimates 2-3%
for 2026-28 mainly to factor in 2Q26 GPM miss. We keep our target price
unchanged at NT$460 based on 2H26-1H27E 17x P/E. We reiterate our Buy on
Eclat as we see fundamentals improving marginally h/h, with input cost pressure
starting to ease and order visibility remaining healthy at 6 months. Meanwhile,
valuation looks increasingly attractive – the stock is trading at 11.5x our NTM EPS,

new the low end of its historical range of 11-34x. With fundamentals turning more
favorable, we see limited downside from current levels. In addition, a 6% dividend
yield provides further downside support while investors wait for earnings
momentum to strengthen.

### Page 5

Eclat (1476.TW)
18 August 2026 Citi Research
5
Eclat
Company description
Founded in 1977, Eclat is a niche apparel ODM/OEM with a unique vertical
integration model. Eclat specializes in high-end functional fabrics and
garments for active wear brands. Its largest brand customers include Nike,
Target, lululemon, and UA. In 2024, Eclat's garment capacity was ~80% in
Vietnam and ~20% in Cambodia. It is currently ramping up new facilities in
Indonesia.

Investment strategy
We have a Buy rating on Eclat. We like Eclat's strong R&D capability in fabrics
and product innovations, which should give Eclat stronger bargaining power
when negotiating tariffs with customers, supporting a steady sales/earnings
trend vs. other suppliers. We expect ongoing strong order strength from
emerging brands, along with new customer acquisition, to drive sales growth
of 9% y/y in 2026E, while product mix upgrade, positive FX, and rising

efficiency should boost solid margin expansion, driving a record EPS in
2026E. We view current risk/reward as attractive and expect a margin
recovery to serve as a positive catalyst.
Valuation
Our target price for Eclat of NT$460 is based on 17x 2H26H-1H27E EPS, 15%
below its historical average in view of persisting uncertainties on
consumption trend and oil price fluctuations.
Risks
Key downside risks that could prevent the shares from reaching our target

Guide to Citi Research Fundamental Research Investment Ratings:
Citi Research stock recommendations include an investment rating and an optional risk rating to highlight high risk stocks.

Risk rating takes into account both price volatility and fundamental criteria. Stocks will either have no risk rating or a High risk
rating assigned.
Investment Ratings: Citi Research investment ratings are Buy, Neutral and Sell. Our ratings are a function of analyst
expectations of expected total return ("ETR") and risk. ETR is the sum of the forecast price appreciation (or depreciation) plus
the dividend yield for a stock within the next 12 months.  The target price is based on a 12 month time horizon. The Investment

rating definitions are: Buy (1) ETR of 15% or more or 25% or more for High risk stocks; and Sell (3) for negative ETR. Any covered
stock not assigned a Buy or a Sell is a Neutral (2). For stocks rated Neutral (2), if an analyst believes that there are insufficient
valuation drivers and/or investment catalysts to derive a positive or negative investment view, they may elect with the approval
of Citi Research management not to assign a target price and, thus, not derive an ETR. Citi Research may suspend its rating

and target price and assign “Rating Suspended” status for regulatory and/or internal policy reasons. Citi Research may also
suspend its rating and target price and assign "Under Review" status for other exceptional circumstances (e.g. lack of
information critical to the analyst's thesis, trading suspension) affecting the company and/or trading in the company's
securities. In both such situations, the rating and target price will show as “—“ and “-“ respectively in the rating history price

chart. Prior to 11 April 2022 Citi Research assigned “Under Review” status to both situations and prior to 11 Nov 2020 only in
exceptional circumstances. As soon as practically possible, the analyst will publish a note re-establishing a rating and
investment thesis. Investment ratings are determined by the ranges described above at the time of initiation of coverage, a
change in investment and/or risk rating, or a change in target price (subject to limited management discretion). At times, the

expected total returns may fall outside of these ranges because of market price movements and/or other short-term volatility
or trading patterns. Such interim deviations will be permitted but will become subject to review by Research Management.
Your decision to buy or sell a security should be based upon your personal investment objectives and should be made only
after evaluating the stock's expected performance and risk.
Catalyst Watch/Short Term Views (“STV”) Ratings Disclosure:

Catalyst Watch and STV Upside/Downside calls: Citi Research may also include a Catalyst Watch or STV Upside or Downside
call to indicate the analyst expects the share price to rise (fall) in absolute terms over a specified period of 30 or 90 days in
reaction to one or more specific near-term catalysts or events impacting the company or the market. A Catalyst Watch will be
published when Analyst confidence is high that an impact to share price will occur; it will be a STV when confidence level is

moderate. A Catalyst Watch or STV Upside/Downside call will automatically expire at the end of the specified 30/90 day
period. The Catalyst Watch will also be automatically removed if share price performance (calculated at market close) exceeds
15% against the direction of the call (unless over-ridden by the analyst). The analyst may also remove a Catalyst Watch or STV
call prior to the end of the specified period in a published research note. A Catalyst Watch/STV Upside or Downside call may be

## Document 149: 260818_ubs_allring

### Page 1

ab 18 August 2026
Global Research
All Ring Tech
CoWoS momentum builds; CPO adds long-term
upside
Stronger 2027 outlook from accelerating CoWoS industry expansion
Versus a month ago when we initiated coverage, we believe All Ring's business visibility
for H226 and 2027 has improved further amid more aggressive industry-wide CoWoS
expansion plans. In Q2 earnings, both ASE and TSMC raised their capex guidance with
ASE indicating 75% of its 2026 US$6.5bn machinery capex guidance will be allocated to

leading edge solutions, which we believe CoWoS is the major part. Based on our
analysis, we think the industry is on track to reach >250kwpm CoWoS capacity by the
end of 2027, up from 160kwpm by the end of 2026. TSMC will continue to expand its
own CoW capacity while also beginning to outsource CoW to ASE/SPIL in 2027 to meet
the growing Cloud AI demand. All Ring stands as a direct beneficiary of larger expansion
plans. We forecast CoWoS sales to grow 77%/31% for All Ring in 2026/27E, accounting

for 91%/75% of total sales.
CPO remains a compelling future growth opportunity
All Ring remains committed to CPO tool development, and continues to make progress.
We forecast CPO to contribute 12%/23% of total sales in 2027/28E. While All Ring is a
latecomer to FAU coupling equipment, we believe the company has been collaborating
closely with end customers and its key OSAT partner SPIL. With All Ring’s strong
execution, its tools have been demonstrating competitive throughput and coupling

performance vs its major competitor. At the upcoming Taiwan SEMICON event in first
week of September, All Ring should also be showcasing its  new CPO offering in addition
to its FAU coupling equipment.
Steadier Q2 gross margin; H226 outlook favorable
Q2 revenue of NT$2.35bn was  up 67% QoQ, in line with UBSe and 1% ahead of Street
consensus. Gross margin came in strong at 54.8%, above UBSe of 52.5% while Op
margin of 33.3% was slightly lower vs UBSe of 35.0%  primarily due to continued higher

R&D and sales & marketing. Looking ahead, we forecast Q3 sales to grow 29% QoQ
with GM sustaining at 54.8% thanks to ongoing strength in CoWoS orders. Q4 sales
should be slightly lower given typical company seasonality; we forecast a 15% QoQ
sales decline, with gross margin moderating to 53.0% for Q4. Overall we expect
2026/27E sales to grow 75% / 59%.
Valuation: reiterate Buy, raise target to NT$1,700
Post Q2 earnings results, we lift our 2026/2027E  EPS by 8%/6% to reflect better CoWoS

opportunity and higher gross margins. We lift our price target to NT$1,700 based on
27x average 2027-28E PE (from 25x prior), supported by a long-term earnings CAGR of
33%. We maintain our Buy rating.

Forecast returns

Company Description
Founded in 1996, All Ring is a Taiwan-based supplier of automation equipment for the
semiconductor back-end packaging and passive component manufacturing industries. The
company's products include underfill dispensers, heatsink placement and automated optical
inspection (AOI) tools for advanced packaging equipment, particularly for heterogeneous

integration. Revenue is generated primarily from sales of semiconductor and electronic
manufacturing equipment and related automation solutions, with more than 90% of
revenue derived from Taiwan.
Valuation Method and Risk Statement
We value All Ring based on PE methodology.
Risks for the semis industry:
- Geopolitical uncertainties: Geopolitical uncertainties including tariffs and more export
control could negatively impact semis cycle, and a downturn would lead to slower demand in

the overall market and moderating capacity expansion.
- Meaningful deceleration of new technology development: Slower demand and less
investment are expected If there is meaningful deceleration of new technology development
such as Cloud AI, autonomous driving, etc.
- Rising utility cost: Larger-than-expected utility cost increases would further pressure
profitability. Rising utility costs in Taiwan have negatively impacted margins for Taiwan tech

companies.
Company risks:
- Faster- or slower-than-expected CoWoS capacity expansion: This could result in All Ring
gaining/losing equipment orders from major foundries and OSATs.
- Competitors achieving technological advancement: Potential risk of competition (from
either local or foreign companies) achieving technological advancements could result in All
Ring losing market share from major foundries and OSATs.
- Better- or worse-than-expected AI growth: Better- or worse-than-expected AI growth could

lead to a ramp-up or a slowdown of equipment orders from major foundries and OSATs to All
Ring, affecting long-term revenue growth potential.
- Slowdown or delay of new technological advancement: A potential slowdown or delay of
the rollout for technological advancements for technologies such as SoIC, CoPoS, and CPO,
would limit future equipment orders.
- Increase of raw material costs: Rising raw material costs may directly impact All Ring’s
margins for equipment shipments.

future results. Transaction costs may be significant in option strategies calling for multiple purchases and sales of options, such as spreads and straddles. Because

Investing in structured investments involves significant risks. For a detailed discussion of the risks involved in investing in any particular structured investment, you must read

the relevant offering materials for that investment. Structured investments are unsecured obligations of a particular issuer with returns linked to the performance of an
underlying asset. Depending on the terms of the investment, investors could lose all or a substantial portion of their investment based on the performance of the underlying
asset. Investors could also lose their entire investment if the issuer becomes insolvent. UBS does not guarantee in any way the obligations or the financial condition of any

issuer or the accuracy of any financial information provided by any issuer. Structured investments are not traditional investments and investing in a structured investment is
not equivalent to investing directly in the underlying asset. Structured investments may have limited or no liquidity, and investors should be prepared to hold their investment
to maturity. The return of structured investments may be limited by a maximum gain, participation rate or other feature. Structured investments may include call features and,

if a structured investment is called early, investors would not earn any further return and may not be able to reinvest in similar investments with similar terms. Structured
investments include costs and fees which are generally embedded in the price of the investment. The tax treatment of a structured investment may be complex and may differ
from a direct investment in the underlying asset. UBS and its employees do not provide tax advice. Investors should consult their own tax advisor about their own tax situation

before investing in any securities.

would be consistent with its investment objective and other principal investment strategies. The returns on a portfolio incorporating ESG factors or sustainable investing
considerations may be lower or higher than portfolios where ESG factors, exclusions, or other sustainability issues are not considered by the portfolio manager, and the
investment opportunities available to such portfolios may differ.

長榮航太 (2645 TT)  Buy

無人機、空中巴士新訂單，有望成為 2027 年營運新動能。  
投資建議 ：考量 2026 年長榮航太的維修業務持續擴張， 2H26 製
造業務有望受惠波音交機量持續提升而改善，並隨著近期立法
院通過無人機預算後，有利過往已爭取到無人機標案的長榮航
太再度取得相關訂單，貢獻 2027 年的營運，故給予長榮航太
Buy 的投資建議，目標價 249 元 (27.4PER X 4Q26-3Q27F EPS) 。 
報載藍全力支持國防無人機產業 634 億預算全數通過： 國民黨
團表示，針對經濟部 6 年新台幣 442 億元「無人載具產業發展
統籌型計畫」，及行政院各部會編列的 192 億元採購預算，合
計 634 億元全數通過。並要求經濟部將預算 「分 6 年平均編列，
每年不得超標」。  
在之前立法院正對於無人機預算進行細節討論後，立法院已通
過國民黨版無人機預算，並以年度預算方式執行，整體符合研
究部先前觀點，後續研究部認為在預算通過後，預計 2027 年相
關無人機標案將開出，貢獻得標廠商 2027 年起的營運成長。而
長榮航太作為先前已得標總價 4.23 億元的軍用商規 -艦載監偵
型無人機廠商，透過先前得標經驗有望取得更多無人機訂單，
貢獻後續營運。  
2027 年空中巴士首件交付、無人機新訂單，有望成為中長期營
運新動能： 2027 年長榮航太除了持續受惠維修業務與波音交機
的逐年擴張之外，在新訂單的部分，先前與空中巴士的新訂單，
預估 1Q27 將迎來首件交付，並待後續取得特殊製程認證後，
2H27 有望迎來新訂單的量產，以及無人機訂單也有望成為長榮
航太新一波的營運動能，預估 2027 年營收 213.81 億元，
YoY+7.04% ，稅後純益 36.52 億元， YoY+19.20% ，EPS 9.75 元。

報載藍全力支持國防無人機產業 634 億預算全數通過：  
國民黨團表示，針對經濟部 6 年新台幣 442 億元 「無人載具產業發展統籌型
計畫」，及行政院各部會編列的 192 億元採購預算，合計 634 億元全數通
過。並要求經濟部將預算「分 6 年平均編列，每年不得超標」。  
在之前立法院正對於無人機預算進行細節討論後，立法院已通過國民黨版
無人機預算，並以年度預算方式執行，整體符合研究部先前觀點，後續研究
部認為在預算通過後，預計 2027 年相關無人機標案將開出，貢獻得標廠商
2027 年起的營運成長。而長榮航太作為先前已得標總價 4.23 億元的軍用商
規 -艦載監偵型無人機廠商，透過先前得標經驗有望取得更多無人機訂單，
貢獻後續營運。  
2027 年空中巴士首件交付、無人機新訂單，有望成為中長期營運新動能：  
2027 年長榮航太除了持續受惠維修業務與波音交機的逐年擴張之外，在新
訂單的部分，先前與空中巴士的新訂單，預估 1Q27 將迎來首件交付，並待
後續取得特殊製程認證後， 2H27 有望迎來新訂單的量產，以及無人機訂單
也有望成為長榮航太新一波的營運動能，預估 2027 年營收 213.81 億元，
YoY+7.04% ，稅後純益 36.52 億元， YoY+19.20% ， EPS 9.75 元。  
投資建議：  
考量 2026 年長榮航太的維修業務持續擴張， 2H26 製造業務有望受惠波音
交機量持續提升而改善，並隨著近期立法院通過無人機預算後，有利過往已
爭取到無人機標案的長榮航太再度取得相關訂單，貢獻 2027 年的營運，故
給予長榮航太 Buy 的投資建議 ， 目標價249 元 (27.4PER X 4Q26-3Q27F EPS) 。

7 群益投顧 
  【投資評等說明】  
  評等  定義  
  強力買進 (Strong Buy)  首次評等潛在上漲空間 ≥ 35%  
  買進 (Buy)  15% ≤首次評等潛在上漲空間 <35%  
  區間操作 (Trading Buy) 5% ≤首次評等潛在上漲空間 <15%

中立 (Neutral)  
無法由基本面給予投資評等  
預期近期股價將處於盤整  
建議降低持股

【投資評等調整機制】  
  投資評等係「首次」給予特定個股投資評等時，其前一日收盤價相較 3 個月
目標價之潛在上漲空間計算而得。個股投資評等分為四個等級，定義如上。
爾後的投資評等係依循「首次評等」，直到停止推薦。   
「強力買進」、「買進」及「區間操作」均有 upside 目標價。差別僅在於，
首次評等時潛在報酬率不同。「中立」則無目標價。  
一旦我們給予特定個股「強力買進」 、 「買進」或「區間操作」之投資評等，
就是責任的開始，爾後將透過各式研究報告作定期性、持續性追蹤基本面及
股價變化，直到停止推薦。  
停止推薦情境：  
1.  達目標價。  
2.  雖未達原訂目標價，但檢視基本面、訊息面、籌碼面等多方訊息，研
判股價上漲空間已然有限，將適時出具降評報告。  
3.  推薦後股價不漲反跌。

【免責聲明】  
  本研究報告僅提供予特定人之客戶作為參考資料「非經同意不得轉載」 。我
們並不確保此資訊的完整性與正確性，投資人應了解，報告中有關未來預測
之陳述可能不會實現，因而不應被依賴。而且此報告並非根據特定投資目的
或依預定對象之財務狀況所撰寫出來的，因此，此研究報告的目的，既非對
投資人於買賣證券、選擇權、期貨或其他證券相關之衍生性商品提供詢價服
務，亦非作為進行交易的要約。投資人應注意到相關證券之價值及收益，可
能會有無預警地上升或下降，產生投資回報金額可能比原始投資來得少的
情形。

預期 2027 年 ASIC 營收將超過 GPU 
 2Q26 毛利率 32.57%，優於預期，2Q26 EPS 24.37 元:奇鋐 2Q26 
EPS 24.37 元，優於元富預估的 22.03 元，獲利達成率 111%。2Q26
營收 491.21 億元，+0%QOQ，因處於平台轉換期，2Q26 Server 佔
比 66%(營收+116%YOY)。2Q26 毛利率 32.57%，優於元富預估的
30.20%，主要是產品組合優化及導入自動化，2Q26 EPS 24.37 元。 
 水冷滲透率越來越高，預期 2027 年 ASIC 營收將超過 GPU:3Q26
將開始出貨少量 VR200 及 ASIC 水冷產品，4Q26 放量，3Q26 及
4Q26 營收動能均為雙位數 QOQ 增長，2027 年隨 ASIC 機種放量，
推估奇鋐於 ASIC 機種出貨品項將多於 GPU 客戶 ， 故以奇鋐出ASIC
單櫃散熱產值來看亦將優於 GPU 客戶，預期 2027 年 ASIC 營收將
首度超過 GPU， 成長動能可期 。隨產品組合優化及自動化程度提升，
預期 2H26 毛利率將優於 2Q26，2027 年毛利率亦優於 2026 年。目
前奇鋐舊廠產能均為滿載，越南新廠產能逐步爬升稼動率， 2027 年
因應客戶訂單需求將擴增水冷各產品線產能約+50%，資本支出續增。 
 維持買進評等，目標價 4250 元：水冷滲透率持續提升 ，2027 年 ASIC
營收將超過 GPU， 營收及毛利率方向均樂觀 ， 故維持買進 ， 預估2026
年 EPS 102.39 元，2027 年 EPS 157.75 元，目標價 4250 元。

水冷滲透率越來越高，預期 2027
年 ASIC 營收將超過 GPU

水冷滲透率越來越高，預期 2027 年 ASIC 營收將超過 GPU 
展望 3Q26，七月營收 185.9 億元，+5.5%MOM，+57.39%YOY，預期八月及
九月營收續強，3Q26 將開始出貨少量 VR200 及 ASIC 水冷產品，預期 4Q26
放量，故 4Q26 營 收 動 能 亦 為 雙 位 數QOQ 增長，元富預估 3Q26 營收
+10~15%QOQ，4Q26 營收+10~15%QOQ，2027 年隨 ASIC 機種放量，推估
奇鋐於 ASIC 機種出貨品項將多於 GPU 客戶，故以奇鋐出 ASIC 單櫃散熱產值
來看亦將優於 GPU 客戶，預期 2027 年 ASIC 營收將首度超過 GPU，成長動能
可期。獲利部分，隨產品組合優化及自動化程度提升，預期2H26 毛利率將優於
2Q26，2027 年毛利率亦優於 2026 年。

針對擴產計劃來看，由於目前奇鋐舊廠產能均為滿載，越南新廠產能逐步爬升
稼動率，2027 年因應客戶訂單需求將擴增水冷各產品線產能約 +50%，2026 年
奇鋐資本支出為 150 億元，2027 年資本支出將高於 2026 年，長線成長動能可
期。關於雙相式液冷，公司認為散熱不是每個案子都用最新技術，將視案件實
際需求為主，以下一世代機種來看，TDP 權衡結果仍採用單相式為主，將持續
跟客戶討論雙相式應用。

維持買進，目標價 4250 元

維持買進評等，目標價 4250 元 
水冷滲透率持續提升，預期 2027 年 ASIC 營收將超過 GPU，營收及毛利率方
向均樂觀，故維持買進，預估 2026 年 EPS 102.39 元，2027 年 EPS 157.75
元，目標價 4250 元。

風險提示 
出貨不如預期，ASP 不如預期。

個股報告 
聯亞(3081) 
矽光需求續強、2027年擴產幅度再放大，上修獲利預估 
投資評等 目標價 
買進 $3825 
前次投資建議：2026.08.03 
「逢低買進」 ，目標價: $2325元

報告日期：2026.08.13 研 究 員：李彝安

摘 要 及 調 整 原 因 
➢ 聯亞 26Q2 營收、毛利率及營益率續創新高，7 月營收突破 5 億元，公司指引 26Q3 營收季
增可達兩成以上，因此上修 2026 年營收、毛利率與 EPS 至 21.79 元；預期 2027 年整體產
能較 2026 年增加約 150%，上修 2027 年營收至 140.49 億元、EPS至 59.76 元；評價維持
「買進」，目標價上修至 3,825 元(64× 2027(F) PER)，上漲空間 40.4%。。

基本資料 結論與建議 
2026/08/12 收盤價： 2725.0 ➢ 26Q2 營收 12.21 億元，季增 35.0%、年增 120.8%，
毛利率 57.5%，較上季增加 2.7 個百分點，營益率
46.9%， 較上季增加5.6 個百分點 ， 稅後淨利4.68 億元、
EPS 4.62 元，營收與獲利續創新高。

➢ 成長動能仍集中於資料中心用 CW Laser，26Q2 占營
收約 85–90%，1.6T矽光需求持續放量。現階段主要限
制來自產能而非需求，前段磊晶與曝光設備持續擴充，
後段則透過增加合作代工廠家數及既有夥伴擴產提高
供給能力。

➢ 2027 年整體產能預計較 2026 年增加約 1.5倍，較先前
增加逾 100%的規劃再上修；隨前段 MOCVD、曝光設
備及後段代工產能同步擴充，加上 1.6T 出貨持續放量
及 3.2T、新規格產品逐步貢獻，研究部上修2027 年營
收至 140.49 億元、年增 146.6%。

➢ 研究部預估 2026 年營收 56.98億元、年增 158.7%，
毛利率 58.3%，稅後淨利 22.17 億元、EPS 21.79 元；
2027 年營收 140.49億元、年增 146.6%，毛利率
61.8%，稅後淨利 60.82 億元、EPS 59.76 元。上修目
標價至 3,825 元(64x2027(F)PER)，潛在上漲空間
40.4%，維持「買進」評等。

資料來源：CMoney；兆豐投顧預估

營 運 分 析 
垂直整合 InP 磊晶至雷射晶片，台灣的 CW Laser主力供應商 
聯亞光電成立於 1997 年,為台灣第一家專業光纖通信用磊晶片製造廠。公司
專注於以砷化鎵(GaAs)與磷化銦(InP)為基板的Ⅲ-Ⅴ族化合物半導體磊晶片
研發與製造,核心技術為有機金屬汽相磊晶(MOCVD),產品為雷射二極體 (LD) 
與檢光器 (PD) 磊晶片,波長涵蓋 650 至 1650nm,廣泛應用於光通訊、資料
中心、消費性光電、工業用雷射與醫療感測等領域。 
聯亞採 Epi House 營運模式,向基板供應商採購  InP 與 GaAs 基板後,於
內部完成 MOCVD 磊晶與部分晶圓前段製程,再出貨予下游後段廠商進行晶
片製程、測試與封裝,最終產品進入光模組廠、系統廠與 CSP 終端客戶。全
球能量產光通訊用雷射磊晶片的廠商不足十家 ,聯亞為其中少數專注於光通
訊與資料中心應用的專業磊晶廠。 
公司產品應用分為三大區塊。資料通訊  (Datacom) 為目前營收絕對主力，
占比 85-90%，其中絕大多數為用於矽光模組之 CW Laser，終端客戶以美
國超大規模雲端服務業者 (Hyperscale CSP) 為主，並透過下游模組廠代工
出貨。電信 (Telecom) 相關產品占營收 5-10%，涵蓋光纖到府 (FTTH)、
長距離 DFB 雷射等傳統電信光源。其他產品約 5%，包括手機應用之雷射
光電二極體 (LDPD)、可見光通訊 (LiFi) 等仍在開發驗證中之新興應用。

- 3 - 
個股報告 
26Q2 毛利率升至 57.5%，營收放大同步推升營益率 
26Q2 營收由前季 9.04億元增至 12.21 億元，季增35.0%，毛利率由 54.8%
升至 57.5%，營益率則由 41.3%提高至 46.9%。毛利率改善受到產品組合及
其他營運因素共同影響，但CW Laser 已量產多年，良率改善空間有限；CW 
Epi 與 CW LD 兩種出貨型態的毛利率差距亦不大，因此本季毛利率上升不宜
歸因於良率提升或出貨型態改變。 
InP 基板價格過去數季明顯上升，但成本增加已透過產品組合及營運策略因
應，並未改變毛利率逐季改善趨勢。考量26Q2 毛利率已升至 57.5%，26H2
矽光產品出貨持續增加，研究部將 2026 年毛利率預估由 55.3%上修至
58.3%。 
除毛利率改善外，營收快速放大亦開始反映營業槓桿。26Q2 營業費用約 1.29
億元，僅較 26Q1 約 1.22 億元小幅增加，使營業費用率由約 13.5%降至
10.6%；同期營益率增加 5.6 個百分點，高於毛利率增加 2.7 個百分點，顯
示營收規模擴大帶來的費用率下降已進一步放大獲利成長。

7 月營收突破 5億元，26H2 出貨續受產能開出帶動 
7 月營收 5.01 億元，月增 19.2%、年增 176.5%，再創單月新高，1–7 月累
計營收 26.27 億元、年增 121.0%。26Q3 營收展望為季增兩成以上，延續近
幾季逐季成長趨勢，需求仍以資料中心矽光 CW Laser 為主要來源。 
研究部預估 26Q3 營收 15.88 億元、季增 30.0%，26Q4 營收 19.84 億元、
季增 25%。其中 26Q3 預估高於目前季增兩成以上的營運展望，主要反映 7
月營收已達 5.01 億元，以及自有產能與後段新增代工產能於 8、9 月持續開
出。 
以 26Q3 營收 15.88 億元計算，8、9 月合計仍需貢獻 10.87 億元，因此後續
月營收表現將直接驗證研究部對後段產能開出速度的假設。若新增代工產能
爬升速度低於預期，26Q3 及全年營收仍存在下修風險。

2027 年產能再擴，成長關鍵由需求轉向供給 
目前 CW Laser需求仍高於可供應產能，2027 年營運成長的關鍵在新增產能
能否依規劃開出並順利轉化為有效產出。先前規劃為 2027 年產能較 2026 年
增加一倍，最新法說則進一步上調至增加約 1.5 倍，並規劃 2028 年延續相
近的擴產速度，以因應主要客戶以三至五年為期的需求規劃。 
研究部因此將 2027 年營收由 91.60 億元大幅上修至 140.5 億元，年增
146.6%。除新增產能帶動出貨量成長外，產品規格升級及 ASP 提升亦挹注
營收，其中1.6T 矽光產品持續放量，更高功率及新規格產品則隨客戶驗證進
度逐步貢獻。後續營收成長能否達成，主要觀察前段設備到位、曝光製程擴
充及後段代工產能能否配合需求同步放大。

曝光與後段代工同步擴充，釋放 CW Laser產能限制 
除了 MOCVD 磊晶設備外，曝光製程亦同為本輪擴產重點。既有 E-beam 設

備之外，第二種曝光技術已投入量產，可大幅提高單機產出效率，後續新增
設備將持續擴大相關產能。第三種半導體級曝光設備則主要為更大尺寸製程
及中長期需求提前布局，正式量產時點較晚，因此研究部暫不將其列入2027
年主要營收成長假設。 
後段 process 採委外模式，隨聯亞出貨需求快速增加，既有代工夥伴產能已
不足以完全支應需求，因此擴充方式以增加合作廠商家數及既有夥伴擴產同
步進行。前段 MOCVD、曝光設備與後段代工能力須同步增加，才能使新增
磊晶產能實際轉化為 CW Laser 出貨。

資料來源：Cmoney；兆豐投顧預估

1.6T放量、產能開出，毛利往六成邁進 
研究部預估聯亞 2026年營收 56.98億元、年增158.7%，毛利率 58.3%，稅
後淨利 22.17億元、EPS 21.79 元；2027 年營收 140.5 億元、年增146.6%，
毛利率 61.8%，稅後淨利 60.8 億元、EPS 59.76 元。 
獲利上修主要反映 26Q2 毛利率及營益率優於原先假設、7 月營收再創新高，
以及 26H2 新增產能持續開出；2027 年則進一步反映整體產能規劃擴大，前
段 MOCVD、曝光設備及後段代工產能同步增加。 
2027 年毛利率預估 61.8%，較 2026 年增加 3.0 個百分點，主要反映高速矽
光產品占比提高、新增產能稼動率提升及製造規模放大後固定成本攤提改
善；營業費用率則隨營收快速成長進一步下降，使營益率改善幅度高於毛利
率。

評等分級：買進（預期未來六個月股價漲幅超過+30%）; 逢低買進（預期未來六個月股價漲幅介於+15%與+30%之間）; 區間操
作（預期未來六個月股價漲跌幅介於-15%與+15%之間）; 逢高賣出（預期未來六個月股價跌幅介於-15%與-30%之間）；賣出（預
期未來六個月股價跌幅超過-30%）。

投資之需求與風險，本公司恕不負擔任何法律責任。任何轉載或引用本報告內容必須經本公司同意。

## Document 170: 3715定穎 統一

Source PDF: all report pdf/3715定穎 統一.pdf
Report source: line報告備份
Date: 2026-08-12
Pages: 7
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

請參見末頁重要批露及免責聲明  1

2026/08/11 投資速報

電子–電子零組件 
定穎投控 (3715.TT) 
AI 認證遞延，靜待重新切入

中立(調降評等) 
出刊緣由：評等調整 
與前次比較： 上次 本次

個股與大盤走勢

近一個月 近三個月 近半年 近一年 
加權指數報酬率 -0.9 8.0 35.8 87.0 
個股股價報酬率 -16.9 -30.0 -11.4 33.8

營收走勢圖

研究員/丁宥霖 yulin.ting@uni-psg.com 
       廖婉婷 TINA_LIAO@uni-psg.com

研究部將定穎投控投資建議由「買進」調降至「中立」 ，主因：(1) 
2Q26 毛利率降至 14.0%， 加計匯兌損失後單季 EPS 為 -0.81 元，
明顯低於原估 0.46 元；(2) 泰國廠 AI 高階產品因量產前最終
版本需重新送樣驗證，量產放量時程由 3Q26 遞延至 4Q26，泰國
廠滿載及單季損益兩平時點亦同步順延至 1Q27；(3) CCL 等原物
料於 2H26 仍有續漲壓力，且成本轉嫁存在時間差，預期 2H26 毛
利率回升幅度有限。綜上，研究部將 2026 年 EPS 預估由 5.42 
元下修至 -0.40 元，2027 年 EPS 預估由 15.11 元下修至 6.47 
元。不過研究部認為，AI PCB 高階產能仍屬稀缺，具備產能規模
且製程能力跟上的板廠，終有機會重新切入供應鏈。評價方面，在 
2026 年轉為虧損、獲利貢獻遞延至 2027 年，且擴產所帶來的折
舊與利息負擔將先行反映的情況下， 現階段評價已屬合理 ，潛在漲
幅空間有限 。 研究部據此將評等調降至 「中立」 ， 並以 2027 年 EPS 
6.47 元、20 倍本益比評價，下修目標價至 130 元。惟若認證進
度提前、AI 出貨時程優於預期，帶動泰國廠稼動率與獲利提前回
升，研究部將重新調升定穎投控之獲利預估、投資評等與目標價。

重點摘要： 
1. 2Q26 營收創歷史新高，惟原物料與匯損侵蝕獲利 
定穎投控 2Q26 營收 68.69 億元 (+22.0% QoQ、+48.4% YoY)，優
於原估的 62.83 億元 ， 成長動能來自汽車板受高階車用板與 ADAS 
需求帶動營收季增 23%、儲存式裝置受 DDR5 需求強勁帶動季增 
31%，網路通訊及伺服器亦季增 24%；產品組合為汽車板 55%、儲
存式裝置 18%、網路通訊及伺服器 11%、顯示面板 9%、電腦及周
邊與其他 6%。 惟毛利率降至 14.0% (-2.1ppt QoQ、-7.5ppt YoY)，
低於原估的 17.2%，主因 CCL 等原物料價格持續上漲。營業費用 
8.95 億元，費用率降至 13.0% (-0.8ppt QoQ) 符合原估，惟毛利
率承壓使營業淨利率降至 1.0% (-1.3ppt QoQ、-7.2ppt YoY)，低
於原估的 4.2%。業外淨支出達 3.89 億元，其中認列匯兌損失 
2.64 億元，主因美元與人民幣對泰銖持續走升。最終 2Q26 稅後
淨損 2.30 億元，EPS 為 -0.81 元，低於原估的 0.46 元。

### Page 2

請參見末頁重要批露及免責聲明  2

圖一、2024~4Q27F 定穎投控產品營收比重

資料來源：定穎投控，統一投顧整理

2. 3Q26 漲價效益逐步顯現，獲利回升至損益兩平之上 
定穎投控 7 月營收 25.31 億元 (+10.5% MoM、+69.7% YoY)，創
單月歷史新高，動能延續自 DDR5 帶動之儲存式裝置、ADAS 帶動
之汽車板，以及網卡與加速卡帶動之網通伺服器，目前各廠區交期
緊俏，在手訂單能見度已逾 3 個月。惟原訂於 3Q26 量產放量之
泰國廠 AI 高階產品因重新送樣驗證而遞延至 4Q26，研究部因而
下修 3Q26 營收預估至 77.65 億元 (+13.0% QoQ、+60.4% YoY)，
原估 87.24 億元。毛利率方面，定穎已於 6 至 7 月完成第二波
產品調漲以反映 2Q26 原物料成本，並表示若 3Q26 上游材料成
本續揚，不排除於 8、9 月採取動態調價；惟 CCL 等原物料 2H26 
預期仍將持續上漲， 且成本轉嫁一般存在時間差，加計原先預期由
高毛利 AI 產品帶動之產品組合改善亦一併遞延，研究部下修  
3Q26 毛利率預估至 15.4% (+1.4ppt QoQ 、-4.0ppt YoY) ，原估 
24.3%，單季 EPS 下修至 0.14 元，原估 2.24 元。

### Page 3

請參見末頁重要批露及免責聲明  3

3. 泰國廠 AI 出貨遞延，損益兩平順延至 1Q27 
受到量產前最終版本樣品需重新送樣驗證影響，定穎投控泰國廠  
AI 高階產品出貨時程遞延 ， 量產放量自原訂之 3Q26 順延至 4Q26 
起陸續出貨，泰國廠滿載與單季損益兩平時點亦由 4Q26 一併遞
延至 1Q27，2026 年底泰國廠佔集團營收比重將降至 20% 以下。
泰國廠目前稼動率僅約 60%，在 AI 出貨遞延、 營收未能同步放大
的情況下，泰國廠虧損收斂速度將明顯慢於原先預期，此亦為本次
下修 2026 年與 2027 年獲利預估的主要原因。研究部認為，AI 
PCB 高階產能仍屬稀缺，具備產能規模且製程能力跟上的板廠， 終
有機會重新切入供應鏈。

圖二、2024~4Q27F 定穎投控產品營收比重

資料來源：定穎投控，統一投顧整理

4. 資本支出倍增至 383 億元，產能布局延伸至 2028 年 
定穎投控於 7 月 6 日及 8 月 7 日兩度公告追加資本支出共計 
199 億元，2026 年資本支出總額自原訂之 184 億元大幅上修至 
383 億元，其中泰國廠區約 238 億元、中國廠區約 145 億元，並
以自有及自籌資金支應。8 月 7 日新增之泰國廠 80.5 億元主要
用於採購長交期設備，以因應 GPU 與 ASIC 客戶需求大幅增加，
新產能預計自 4Q27 起陸續開出，滿載後可新增年產值 120 億
元，帶動泰國廠產值目標自 2027 年的 300 億元進一步提升至 
2028 年的 420 億元。黃石 P3 廠區則追加 99.2 億元，新產能
自 3Q27 起陸續開出，全數開出後年產值可達 100 億元，定位為
供應中系 AI 客戶； 中國既有廠區目前則維持滿載，不含 P3 之最
大月產值約 25 億元。

### Page 4

請參見末頁重要批露及免責聲明  4

5. 下修 2026-2027 年獲利預估，2026 年轉為虧損 
綜上所述，研究部下修定穎投控 2026 年營收預估至 306.16 億
元 (+57.4% YoY)，較原估 309.50 億元小幅下修，主要反映泰國
廠 AI 出貨遞延；2026 年毛利率預估 16.2% (-3.1ppt YoY)，較
原估 22.1% 明顯下修， 主因 CCL 等原物料持續上漲， 且原先預期
由高毛利 AI 產品帶動之產品組合改善一併遞延；在毛利率下修
並加計 1H26 累計 7.24 億元匯兌損失下，2026 年稅後 EPS 由
原估 5.42 元下修至 -0.40 元，轉為虧損。 
展望 2027 年，隨泰國廠自 1Q27 起達滿載並進入完整貢獻年度、
AI 營收佔集團比重提升至 40%，加上黃石 P3 新產能自 3Q27 起
加入貢獻 ， 研究部上修 2027 年營收預估至 483.75 億元 (+58.0% 
YoY)，原估 446.37 億元；惟考量量產初期良率仍處爬坡階段，且
原物料成本續揚將壓抑毛利率表現， 2027 年毛利率預估 20.0% 
(+3.8ppt YoY)，較原估 26.6% 下修，稅後 EPS 預估為 6.47 元 
(原估 15.11 元)。

附錄 一 ： 評 等 之 標 準 
評等  定義  
強力買進     預估未來 6 個月內的絕對報酬超過 25%以上  
買進  預估未來 6 個月內的絕對報酬 10% 以上  
中立  預估未來 6 個月內的絕對報酬介於 10～-10%  
降低持股  預估未來 6 個月內的絕對報酬介於 (-10%)以下  
未評等  沒有足夠的基本資料判斷該公司評等

附 錄 二 ： 免 責 宣 言 
© 2010 統 一 投 資 顧 問 股 份 有 限 公 司 版 權 所 有 。 本 公 司 提 供 之 報 告 內 容 係 根 據 本 公
司 認 可 之 資 料來 源 ， 並 基 於 特 定 日 期 所 做 之 判 斷 ， 但 不 保 證 其 完 整 性 或 正 確 性 ， 報
告 中 所 有 的 意 見 及 預 估 ， 如 有 變 更 恕 不 另 行 通 知 。

本 研 究 報 告 所 載 之 投 資 資 訊 ， 僅 提 供 客 戶 做 為 一 般 投 資 參 考 ， 並 非 針 對 特 定 對 象 提
供 專 屬 之 投 資 建 議 。 文 中 所 載 資 訊 或 任 何 意 見 ， 不 構 成 任 何 買 賣 有 價 證 券 或 其 他 投
資 標 的 之 要 約 、 宣 傳 或 引 誘 等 事 項 。 對 於 本 投 資 報 告 所 討 論 或 建 議 之 任 何 證 券 、 投
資 標 的 ， 或 文 中 所 討 論 或 建 議 之 投 資 策 略 ， 投 資 人 應 就 其 是 否 適 合 本 身 財 務 狀 況 與
投 資 條 件 ， 進 一 步 諮 詢 財 務 顧 問 的 意 見 。 本 投 資 報 告 之 內 容 取 材 自 據 信 為 可 靠 之 資

一. 2Q26 營收 YoY 33.09 %，稅後 EPS 2. 56 元 
緯致 受中國互聯網、遊戲客戶持續成長，以及台灣半導體客戶成
長強勁帶動，營收 36 億元創下單季 歷史 新高， QoQ 6.6 %，YoY 
33.0 %；營業毛利 5.4 億元，QoQ 1 2.0 %，YoY 1 4.9 %，毛利率 15.0 %，

升，營業利益大幅成長，營益率達 6.2 %。營業利益 2.28 億元，稅
後純益 1.83 億元，稅後 EPS 2. 50 元。

7 月份營收 12. 7 億元，MoM 3.2 %，YoY 3 3.4 %；1-7 月累計營收 82.9
億元， YoY 31.2 8%，預期全年營收呈現高雙位數成長 。

二. 預估 毛利率 持平 ，互聯網、半導體為 主要 成長動能  
緯致的營收產業別為互聯網、 高科技 &製造、半導體、育樂、金融
及其他。 隨市場對 AI 轉型需求強勁，預期基礎性、重複性技術需
求逐漸縮減，而對 AI 演算法、數據訓練、垂直大模型研發需求快
速提升，因此 緯致 亦發展資料標註、高品質資料整理及模型訓練
優化等服 務；半導體業務則為近期新切入之領域，為今年度成長
最快速之領域，持續擴大專業團隊，目標為擴大至 300 至 500 位
工程師規模。

按客戶產業區分，互聯網成長動能維持強勁， 第二季互聯網客戶
需求持續放量再加上新客戶顯著貢獻 ，營收年增 68% ，但同時也
稀釋毛利 ，預期 Q3 毛利率將回升但會較去年同期下降 ，但下半年
有望維持雙位數成長 ；遊戲產業則具備長期與穩定獲利潛力， 單
一客戶雖量體相對較小，但利潤空間高，並且資金回收表現穩定，
緯致 目前已在中國多家遊戲龍頭取得重要服務地位，並建立可標
準化、可複製之模式持續拓展新客戶；新能源車部份，則受中國
電動車市場競爭激烈影響，價格與利潤壓力仍在。

貳、公 司 概 況

一. 公司簡介  
緯創軟體股份有限公司（簡稱： 緯致 ；代號： 4953 ）成立於  1992 
年，隸屬緯創集團，是台灣領先的跨國資訊服務與數位轉型解決
方 案提供商 。公司早 期以 軟體外包 、系統開 發與 BPO 業務為 基
礎，近年因應全球  IT 服務市場快速升級，全面轉向  AI、大數據、
雲端、 5G、智慧製造與遊戲開發等高附加價值技術服務領域，逐
步建立差異化競爭力。 緯致 採全球交付模式，在台灣、中國大陸、
日本、歐美及東南亞等地設有據點， 2024 年底員工規模已突破萬
人，服務橫跨互聯網、半導體、金融、製造與新能源等產業，其
中大陸市場仍為主要客戶來源，營收占比約七成，但台灣、日美
市場近年成長動能強勁，為公司降低區域集中度的重要基礎。憑
藉 CMMI Level 5 、ISO27001 等國際級認證及成熟的跨國協作能
力， 緯致 已成為亞洲具備系統整合、技術服務與  AI 應用導入能
力的關鍵  IT 服務供應 商。

二. 營運概況及展望  
市場 概況  
全球資訊服務（ IT Services ）與軟體外包市場自  2020 年以來持續
受企業數位轉型與雲端化需求推動而保持成長，生成式  AI 的快
速落地更進一步重塑企業  IT 支出結構，使 AI、雲端 、數 據成為
企業  IT 投資的核心方向。根據國際研究機構統計，全球資訊服
務 市 場 規 模 自 2020 年約  1 兆 美 元 提 升 至 2024 年 超 過 1.26 
兆美元，年複合增長率約  6%，預估  2025 年仍具穩健擴張空間。
企業導入  AI 不 僅 聚 焦 於 提 升 營 運 效 率 ， 也 加 速 IT 系 統 整 合
（IT/OT convergence ）、資料治理、流程重塑與高階工程服務需求
的上升。此外，遊戲、互聯網與半導體等行業在  AI 加速引擎驅
動下，對工程師投入、 AI 資料建構、模型訓練與自動化開發等專
業 服務的需 求強勁 ，成為 2024 –2025 年帶動資訊 服務市場 的主
要動能之一 。

正從人力規模導向轉向 AI 能力、跨域整合、全球交付的競爭框
